  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c01",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s1",
      "quote": "The aufhocker ( German: [ˈaʊfˌhɔkɐ] ) or huckup ( German: [ˈhʊkˌʔʊp] ) is a shapeshifter in German folklore.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aufhocker atau huckup adalah makhluk pengubah wujud dalam cerita rakyat Jerman (German).",
        "en": "The aufhocker or huckup is a shapeshifter of German folklore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c02",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s1",
      "quote": "The Aufhocker is described in German legend as having an unidentifiable shape because it takes on shapes such as a goblin, a beautiful woman, a dead man, or various animals.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aufhocker digambarkan tak punya bentuk yang dapat dikenali karena mengambil rupa goblin, perempuan cantik, orang mati, atau berbagai hewan.",
        "en": "The Aufhocker is said to have no identifiable shape, taking forms such as a goblin, a beautiful woman, a dead man or various animals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c03",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s1",
      "quote": "The name Aufhocker means \"leap upon\". This name is used because according to legend, the Aufhocker would leap upon the back of its victims which are often thieves and criminals that need to be taught a lesson. Other common targets in the Aufhocker legend are lone travelers and old women.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Aufhocker berarti \"melompat ke atas\", karena menurut legenda ia melompat ke punggung korbannya, sering pencuri dan penjahat yang perlu diberi pelajaran, juga pejalan sendirian dan perempuan tua.",
        "en": "The name Aufhocker means 'leap upon', since legend says it leaps onto victims' backs, often thieves and criminals needing a lesson, also lone travellers and old women."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c04",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s1",
      "quote": "The victims often die of exhaustion because the Aufhocker cannot be shaken off or killed. However, it is believed that travelers can free themselves if they are able to make it home before being killed. These attacks often occur in specific locations such as bridges, crossroads, springs, woods, on a path through a hollow, and cemeteries.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Korban sering mati kelelahan karena Aufhocker tak bisa dienyahkan atau dibunuh, tetapi pejalan bisa bebas bila sempat sampai di rumah; serangan terjadi di jembatan, persimpangan, mata air, hutan, jalan cekung, dan pemakaman.",
        "en": "Victims often die of exhaustion as the Aufhocker cannot be shaken off or killed, but travellers can free themselves by reaching home; attacks occur at bridges, crossroads, springs, woods, hollow paths and cemeteries."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c05",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s1",
      "quote": "Similar myths can be found in most of Europe. In the Charente-Maritime département of France, the Ganipote is depicted as having similar traits.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mitos serupa ditemukan di sebagian besar Eropa (Europe); di Charente-Maritime, Prancis (France), Ganipote digambarkan bersifat serupa.",
        "en": "Similar myths are found across most of Europe; in Charente-Maritime, France, the Ganipote is described with similar traits."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c06",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s2",
      "quote": "Der Aufhocker (niederdeutsch Huckup, sorbisch Bubak) ist in der Mythologie ein koboldartiger Druckgeist, der Wanderern, die nachts noch unterwegs sind, auf die Schultern oder den Rücken springt und mit jedem Schritt schwerer wird. ... Der Aufhocker bleibt auf dem Wanderer sitzen, bis dieser durch das heraufbrechende Licht, ein Gebet oder Glockenläuten von ihm erlöst wird",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aufhocker (Jerman Hilir Huckup, Sorbia Bubak) adalah roh penekan mirip kobold yang melompat ke bahu atau punggung pejalan malam dan makin berat di setiap langkah; ia tetap duduk di sana sampai pejalan dibebaskan oleh cahaya fajar, doa, atau dentang lonceng.",
        "en": "The Aufhocker (Low German Huckup, Sorbian Bubak) is a kobold-like pressure spirit that leaps onto the shoulders or back of night travellers and grows heavier each step; it stays there until the traveller is freed by dawn light, a prayer or bells."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c07",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s2",
      "quote": "Manchmal treten die Aufhocker zunächst als mitleiderregende alte Frauen in Erscheinung; sie können aber auch Tiergestalten wie Bär, Kalb (wie beim Bahkauv), Werwolf (wie beim Stüpp) oder Hund (wie beim Sürthgens Mossel) annehmen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aufhocker kadang muncul sebagai perempuan tua yang memancing iba, tetapi juga bisa berwujud hewan seperti beruang, anak sapi (Bahkauv), manusia serigala (Stüpp), atau anjing.",
        "en": "Aufhockers sometimes first appear as pitiable old women, but can also take animal forms such as a bear, calf (Bahkauv), werewolf (Stüpp) or dog."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c08",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s2",
      "quote": "Typische Spukorte wie Bäche, Brücken, Seen, Wälder, Gräben, Wegkreuzungen, Hohlwege, Kirchhöfe und Mord- oder Richtstätten sind die übliche Stelle für eine Begegnung mit dem Aufhocker",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tempat angker yang khas seperti sungai kecil, jembatan, danau, hutan, parit, persimpangan, jalan cekung, pemakaman, serta tempat pembunuhan atau eksekusi adalah lokasi umum pertemuan dengan Aufhocker.",
        "en": "Typical haunted places such as brooks, bridges, lakes, woods, ditches, crossroads, sunken lanes, churchyards and murder or execution sites are where one usually meets the Aufhocker."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c09",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s2",
      "quote": "Seine Ursprünge hat der Glaube an den Aufhocker jedoch in der Furcht vor dem Wiedergänger, dem Untoten. Die ältesten Berichte über Aufhocker sprechen eindeutig von „aufhuckenden Leichen“ und nicht von Kobolden oder Gespenstern.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kepercayaan akan Aufhocker berakar pada ketakutan terhadap Wiedergänger (mayat hidup); laporan tertua jelas berbicara tentang \"mayat yang melompat ke punggung\" (aufhuckenden Leichen), bukan kobold atau hantu.",
        "en": "Belief in the Aufhocker stems from fear of the Wiedergänger (revenant); the oldest reports clearly speak of 'corpses leaping onto backs' (aufhuckenden Leichen), not kobolds or ghosts."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c10",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s1",
      "quote": "The Aufhocker is a very dangerous theriomorph that is sometimes said to tear out the throats of humans instead of killing its victims from exhaustion. The version of the Aufhocker that attacks victims' throats is linked to vampirism.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aufhocker kadang dikatakan merobek leher manusia alih-alih membunuh korban dengan kelelahan; versi ini dikaitkan dengan vampirisme.",
        "en": "The Aufhocker is sometimes said to tear out human throats rather than exhaust its victims; this version is linked to vampirism."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c11",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s2",
      "quote": "Um solch einen Aufhocker handelt es sich auch bei dem Hackestüpp von Düren, der seine Opfer zunächst als verspieltes Hündchen begleitet, ihnen dann auf den Rücken springt, sich nicht mehr abschütteln lässt und von Schritt zu Schritt schwerer wird.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hackestüpp dari Düren juga Aufhocker: mula-mula menemani korbannya sebagai anak anjing yang lucu, lalu melompat ke punggung mereka, tak bisa dienyahkan, dan makin berat di setiap langkah.",
        "en": "The Hackestüpp of Düren is such an Aufhocker: it first follows victims as a playful puppy, then leaps onto their backs, cannot be shaken off and grows heavier step by step."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c12",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s2",
      "quote": "Das Bahkauv („Bachkalb“) ist eine Aachener Sagengestalt, die nachts betrunkene Männer erschrecken und sie auffordern soll, sie auf ihren Schultern zu tragen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bahkauv (\"anak sapi sungai\") adalah tokoh legenda Aachen (Aachener) yang konon menakuti lelaki mabuk pada malam hari dan meminta digendong di bahu mereka.",
        "en": "The Bahkauv ('brook calf') is an Aachen legend figure said to frighten drunken men at night and demand to be carried on their shoulders."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "aufhocker-c13",
      "source_id": "aufhocker-s2",
      "quote": "Der Wanderer ist wie gelähmt, leidet unter Beklemmung und ist unfähig sich umzuwenden.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pejalan seperti lumpuh, merasa sesak, dan tak mampu menoleh.",
        "en": "The traveller is as if paralysed, feels oppressed and cannot turn around."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Bergmönch (\"biarawan gunung\") adalah roh pertambangan dalam cerita rakyat Jerman, raksasa bertudung biarawan yang menghuni lubang tambang, menghukum penambang jahat dan menolong yang baik.",
    "en": "The Bergmönch ('mountain monk') is a mining spirit of German folklore, a giant in a monk's cowl who haunts the mine shafts, punishing wicked miners and helping good ones.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "bergmonch-c01",
      "bergmonch-c02",
      "bergmonch-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Bergmönch adalah roh gunung dalam cerita rakyat Jerman, juga dikenal sebagai Meister Hämmerling. Ia sebesar raksasa, berambut putih, bermata menyala, dan memakai jubah bertudung hitam seperti biarawan, dari mana namanya berasal; ia juga bisa berpakaian penambang, menjelma kuda berleher panjang, atau menjadi tak terlihat. Artikel bahasa Jerman menyebutnya tokoh yang sangat kontradiktif, kadang dianggap hantu seorang kepala tambang yang tak bisa berpisah dari pekerjaannya.",
      "en": "The Bergmönch is a mountain spirit of German folklore, also known as Meister Hämmerling. He is giant-sized, white-haired and fiery-eyed, wearing a black hooded cowl like a monk, which gave him his name; he may also dress as a miner, turn into a long-necked horse or become invisible. The German article calls him a deeply contradictory figure, sometimes held to be the ghost of a mine master who could not part with his work.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bergmonch-c01",
        "bergmonch-c02",
        "bergmonch-c07",
        "bergmonch-c08",
        "bergmonch-c11"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia tinggal di lubang dan terowongan tambang di pegunungan, sibuk terutama pada hari Jumat memindahkan bijih dari satu ember ke ember lain. Ia mudah marah dan berbahaya: napasnya beracun dan dapat membunuh dua belas orang sekaligus, dan siapa pun yang mengolok-olok kerjanya yang sia-sia akan dihukum. Ia juga dapat meruntuhkan terowongan atau menimbulkan banjir dan ledakan gas tambang.",
      "en": "He lives in the pits and galleries of mountain mines and is busiest on Fridays, shifting ore from one bucket to another. He is hot-tempered and dangerous: his poisonous breath can kill twelve people at once, and anyone who mocks his pointless labour is punished. He can also bring galleries down or cause floods and firedamp.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bergmonch-c03",
        "bergmonch-c04",
        "bergmonch-c08",
        "bergmonch-c10"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Namun ia juga bisa adil dan suka menolong, melindungi penambang yang baik dan menghukum yang jahat, seperti mandor yang kejam, serta kebiasaan bersiul, mengumpat, egois, dan malas. Ia memberi minyak lampu yang tak pernah habis selama rahasianya dijaga, menggali bijih bagi penambang kesayangannya, dan menunjukkan urat emas dan perak.",
      "en": "Yet he can also be fair and helpful, guarding good miners and punishing bad ones, such as a cruel foreman, along with whistling, cursing, selfishness and idleness. He gives lamp oil that never runs out as long as the secret is kept, digs ore for his favourite miners and shows them hidden veins of gold and silver.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bergmonch-c05",
        "bergmonch-c06",
        "bergmonch-c09"
      ]
    }
  ],

    "en": "Lamp oil from the Bergmönch was said to last for years without running out, so long as the miner never revealed where it came from.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c01",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s1",
      "quote": "The Bergmönch (\"mountain monk\"; German: [ˈbɛʁkˌmœnç] ) is a mountain spirit from German folklore. He is also known as Meister Hämmerling (\"master hammering guy\").",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bergmönch (\"biarawan gunung\") adalah roh gunung dari cerita rakyat Jerman (German), juga dikenal sebagai Meister Hämmerling.",
        "en": "The Bergmönch ('mountain monk') is a mountain spirit of German folklore, also called Meister Hämmerling."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c02",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s1",
      "quote": "He has white hair and fiery looking eyes as large as dining plates. The mountain spirit wears a black hooded cowl as used by monks; this gave rise to his name Bergmönch. He also can be dressed like a miner. As such he is called Meister Hämmerling. Additionally, he can take the shape of a horse with long neck and terrible looking eyes, and is also able to become invisible.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bergmönch berambut putih, bermata menyala sebesar piring, berjubah bertudung hitam seperti biarawan; ia bisa berpakaian penambang (Meister Hämmerling), menjelma kuda berleher panjang, dan menjadi tak terlihat.",
        "en": "The Bergmönch is white-haired, with fiery plate-sized eyes and a black monk's hooded cowl; he can dress as a miner (Meister Hämmerling), become a long-necked horse and turn invisible."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c03",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s1",
      "quote": "The residence of the Bergmönch are pits and mines in the mountains. There he is active as well deep in the pits as up on the surface. Especially on Fridays this mountain spirit is very busy. He fills the excavated ores from one bucket to another.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bergmönch tinggal di lubang dan tambang di pegunungan, aktif di kedalaman maupun permukaan, terutama sibuk pada hari Jumat (Fridays) memindahkan bijih dari satu ember ke ember lain.",
        "en": "The Bergmönch dwells in mountain pits and mines, active deep down and at the surface, busiest on Fridays, moving ore from one bucket to another."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c04",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s1",
      "quote": "The mountain spirit generally is an erratic and dangerous being. His breath is poisonous, able to kill twelve people at once.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Roh gunung ini umumnya makhluk yang tak menentu dan berbahaya; napasnya beracun dan dapat membunuh dua belas orang sekaligus.",
        "en": "The mountain spirit is generally an erratic, dangerous being; his poisonous breath can kill twelve people at once."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c05",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s1",
      "quote": "The Bergmönch can also be just and helpful, guarding the good miners and punishing the evil ones. He punishes vices such as whistling and cursing, egoism, infidelity and idleness. For example, the mountain spirit killed an evil foreman of miners by crushing his head invisibly with his knees.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bergmönch juga bisa adil dan menolong, melindungi penambang baik dan menghukum yang jahat; ia menghukum kebiasaan bersiul, mengumpat, egois, tidak setia, dan malas, misalnya membunuh mandor jahat dengan meremukkan kepalanya secara tak terlihat.",
        "en": "The Bergmönch can also be just and helpful, guarding good miners and punishing evil ones; he punishes whistling, cursing, egoism, infidelity and idleness, once killing an evil foreman by invisibly crushing his head."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c06",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s1",
      "quote": "This oil never diminishes and burns steadily even for years when the miner never reveals the secret that he got lamp oil from the Bergmönch. He also has favorite miners whose work he does, excavating ores for them.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Minyak lampu pemberiannya tak pernah berkurang dan menyala bertahun-tahun selama penambang tak membuka rahasianya; ia juga mengerjakan pekerjaan penambang kesayangannya, menggali bijih bagi mereka.",
        "en": "The oil he gives never diminishes and burns for years so long as the miner keeps the secret; he also does the work of favourite miners, digging ore for them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c07",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s2",
      "quote": "Besonders der Bergmönch wurde zu einer ausgesprochen widersprüchlichen Figur: anstatt in der Gestalt eines Mönches erscheint er auch als Riese mit Kapuze (mit sprühenden Augen, groß wie Kutschenräder), in der Gestalt eines Steigers oder Bergmeisters (mit einem silbernen Grubenlicht) oder noch häufiger als Geschworener.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bergmönch menjadi tokoh yang sangat kontradiktif: selain sebagai biarawan, ia tampil sebagai raksasa bertudung dengan mata berkilat sebesar roda kereta, sebagai mandor atau kepala tambang (Bergmeister) dengan lampu tambang perak, atau sebagai pengawas tambang.",
        "en": "The Bergmönch became a highly contradictory figure: besides a monk he appears as a hooded giant with sparkling eyes as big as coach wheels, as a foreman or mine master (Bergmeister) with a silver lamp, or as a mine juror."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c08",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s2",
      "quote": "Tatsächlich wird er in manchen Sagen für das Gespenst eines toten Bergmeisters gehalten, der sich nicht von seiner geliebten Arbeit trennen konnte, und nun für immer in den Stollen und Gruben umgehen muss. Noch immer ist er gefährlich, wie ein Bergteufel, und tötet mit seinem Gifthauch die Bergleute, die sich über ihn beschweren.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sebagian legenda ia dianggap hantu kepala tambang (Bergmeister) yang tak bisa berpisah dari pekerjaannya dan kini selamanya bergentayangan di terowongan; ia tetap berbahaya, membunuh dengan napas beracun penambang yang mengeluhkannya.",
        "en": "Some legends hold him to be the ghost of a dead mine master (Bergmeister) unable to leave his beloved work, now haunting the galleries forever; still dangerous, he kills complaining miners with his poisonous breath."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c09",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s1",
      "quote": "He also shows his favourite miners hidden lodes of gold and silver but the miner has to throw some of his mining tools in the offered lode or it will be closed to never being seen by human eyes again.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia menunjukkan urat emas dan perak tersembunyi kepada penambang kesayangannya, tetapi penambang harus melempar sebagian alatnya ke urat itu, atau urat itu akan tertutup selamanya.",
        "en": "He shows favourite miners hidden lodes of gold and silver, but the miner must throw some tools into the lode or it closes, never to be seen again."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c10",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s2",
      "quote": "Er lässt Stollen einstürzen oder verursacht Wassereinbrüche und Schlagwetter.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia meruntuhkan terowongan atau menimbulkan banjir dan ledakan gas tambang.",
        "en": "He makes galleries collapse or causes inrushes of water and firedamp."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergmonch-c11",
      "source_id": "bergmonch-s1",
      "quote": "The appearance of the Bergmönch is like a giant in size.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bergmönch berukuran seperti raksasa.",
        "en": "The Bergmönch is giant-like in size."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Bergsrå (\"rå gunung\") adalah roh atau makhluk gunung dalam kepercayaan rakyat Nordik yang menguasai bijih di dalam gunung dan memikat manusia masuk ke kediamannya.",
    "en": "The Bergsrå ('mountain rå') is a mountain spirit of Nordic folk belief that rules the ore within the mountain and lures people into its dwelling.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "bergsra-c01",
      "bergsra-c02",
      "bergsra-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Bergsrå, juga disebut bergatrollet (\"troll gunung\") atau bergakungen (\"raja gunung\"), adalah makhluk gaib dalam kepercayaan rakyat Nordik yang tinggal di dalam gunung dan menguasai bijih logamnya. Ia bisa laki-laki maupun perempuan, dan dalam banyak cerita dikelilingi keluarga serta istana para troll.",
      "en": "The Bergsrå, also called bergatrollet ('mountain troll') or bergakungen ('mountain king'), is a supernatural being of Nordic folk belief who lives inside the mountain and rules over its ore. It could be male or female, and in many tales is surrounded by its family and a court of trolls.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bergsra-c01",
        "bergsra-c02",
        "bergsra-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Bergsrå sering mengacaukan penglihatan orang yang kebetulan menemukan endapan bijih sehingga mereka tak bisa menemukannya lagi. Ia juga terkenal memikat manusia masuk ke gunung; orang yang diambil itu disebut bergtagen (\"diambil ke dalam gunung\"), dan hubungan antara manusia dan bergsrå lama dianggap nyata.",
      "en": "The Bergsrå often confuses the sight of people who chance upon ore deposits so that they can never find them again. It is also known for luring humans into the mountain; someone taken this way was called bergtagen ('taken into the mountain'), and dealings between humans and a bergsrå were long thought to be real.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bergsra-c02",
        "bergsra-c04",
        "bergsra-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pada 1691 seorang buruh tani remaja, Sven Andersson dari Västergötland, dijatuhi hukuman mati karena mengaku bersetubuh dengan bergsrå perempuan. Ia bercerita pernah tertidur di hutan dekat sebuah gunung saat mencari kambing yang lepas, lalu dibawa masuk ke gunung oleh seorang perempuan berbaju putih yang memberinya makan dan minum.",
      "en": "In 1691 a teenage farmhand, Sven Andersson of Västergötland, was sentenced to death for confessing to intercourse with a female bergsrå.       ]

    {
      "id": "bergsra-s2",
      "url": "https://sv.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bergsr%C3%A5et",
      "title": "Bergsrået",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (sv)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "sv",

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "bergsra-c01",
      "source_id": "bergsra-s1",
      "quote": "The Bergsrå (Mountain Rå), Bergatrollet (Mountain Troll), or Bergakungen (Mountain King) was a mythical creature or spirit of the mountain in Norse mythology.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bergsrå (Rå Gunung), Bergatrollet (Troll Gunung), atau Bergakungen (Raja Gunung) adalah makhluk mitis atau roh gunung dalam mitologi Nordik (Norse).",
        "en": "The Bergsrå (Mountain Rå), Bergatrollet (Mountain Troll) or Bergakungen (Mountain King) was a mythical creature or mountain spirit in Norse mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergsra-c02",
      "source_id": "bergsra-s1",
      "quote": "The bergsrå could be either masculine or feminine. It lived in the mountain with a court of relatives and sometimes surrounded by trolls. It was a common phenomenon in the mythology about the bergsrå to trick people into their dwellings in the mountain and to become bergtagen",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bergsrå bisa laki-laki atau perempuan, tinggal di gunung bersama istana kerabat dan kadang dikelilingi troll; ia lazim memperdaya orang masuk ke kediamannya di gunung sehingga mereka menjadi bergtagen (\"diambil ke dalam gunung\").",
        "en": "The bergsrå could be male or female, living in the mountain with a court of relatives and sometimes trolls; it commonly tricked people into its mountain home so they became bergtagen ('taken into the mountain')."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergsra-c03",
      "source_id": "bergsra-s1",
      "quote": "A typical description of such a claimed occurrence was given by Sven Andersson in 1691 when he was on trial for having sexual intercourse with a female bergsrå.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gambaran khas kejadian semacam itu diberikan Sven Andersson pada 1691 ketika ia diadili karena bersetubuh dengan bergsrå perempuan.",
        "en": "A typical account was given by Sven Andersson in 1691 when tried for intercourse with a female bergsrå."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergsra-c04",
      "source_id": "bergsra-s2",
      "quote": "Bergsrået, eller bergatrollet eller bergakungen, var i nordisk folktro ett övernaturligt väsen, som bor i berget och råder över dess malm. Bergsrået förvänder ofta synen på dem som råkat upptäcka malmfyndigheter, så att de senare inte kan hitta dem. I många berättelser omges bergsrået av sin familj och ett hov av troll.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bergsrået (bergatrollet, bergakungen) dalam kepercayaan rakyat Nordik (nordisk) adalah makhluk gaib yang tinggal di gunung dan menguasai bijihnya; ia sering mengacaukan penglihatan orang yang menemukan endapan bijih sehingga tak bisa menemukannya lagi, dan dalam banyak cerita dikelilingi keluarga dan istana troll.",
        "en": "Bergsrået (bergatrollet, bergakungen) in Nordic (nordisk) folk belief is a supernatural being living in the mountain and ruling its ore; it often twists the sight of those who find ore deposits so they cannot find them again, and in many tales is surrounded by family and a court of trolls."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergsra-c05",
      "source_id": "bergsra-s2",
      "quote": "Umgänge mellan människor och bergrå var något som långt fram i tiden sågs som en realitet: en människa som hade gjort detta blev tagen in i berget, något som kallades bergtagning och var ett känt fenomen inom mytologin kring bergsrået.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hubungan antara manusia dan bergrå lama dianggap nyata; orang yang melakukannya diambil ke dalam gunung, disebut bergtagning, fenomena terkenal dalam mitologi bergsrå.",
        "en": "Dealings between humans and a bergrå were long seen as real; one who had them was taken into the mountain, called bergtagning, a known phenomenon in bergsrå lore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergsra-c06",
      "source_id": "bergsra-s2",
      "quote": "År 1691 dömdes en dräng, Sven Andersson från Vättle härad i Västergötland, till döden för att ha haft samlag med ett kvinnligt bergrå .",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 1691 buruh tani Sven Andersson dari Vättle, Västergötland, dijatuhi hukuman mati karena bersetubuh dengan bergrå perempuan.",
        "en": "In 1691 farmhand Sven Andersson of Vättle hundred in Västergötland was sentenced to death for intercourse with a female bergrå."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bergsra-c07",
      "source_id": "bergsra-s2",
      "quote": "Drängen hade förklarat att han en gång somnat i skogen vid ett berg då han letat efter en bortsprungen get och då tagits in i berget av en kvinna i vitt. Kvinnan hade gett honom mat och dryck och sedan haft samlag med honom.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Buruh tani itu mengaku pernah tertidur di hutan dekat gunung saat mencari kambing yang lepas, lalu dibawa ke dalam gunung oleh perempuan berbaju putih yang memberinya makan dan minum, lalu bersetubuh dengannya.",
        "en": "The farmhand said he had once fallen asleep in the forest by a mountain while seeking a stray goat and was taken into the mountain by a woman in white, who gave him food and drink and then lay with him."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

    {
      "id": "blain-c01",
      "source_id": "blain-s1",
      "quote": "Blainn (altnordisch Bláinn) ist in der nordischen Dichtung ein Begriff, der auch für mythische Wesen verwendet wurde.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Blainn (Nordik Kuno: Bláinn) adalah istilah dalam puisi Nordik (nordischen) yang juga dipakai untuk makhluk mitis.",
        "en": "Blainn (Old Norse Bláinn) is a term in Norse (nordischen) poetry also used for mythical beings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "blain-c02",
      "source_id": "blain-s1",
      "quote": "Er wurde sowohl als Umschreibung für den Vorzeitriesen Ymir als auch für Zwerge gebraucht. Das Wort konnte aber auch zur Umschreibung von nichtmythischen Dingen verwendet werden, wie zum Beispiel einem Teil des Himmels.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Istilah ini dipakai sebagai sebutan bagi raksasa purba Ymir maupun kurcaci, dan juga untuk hal nonmitis seperti bagian langit.",
        "en": "It was used as a byname both for the primeval giant Ymir and for dwarfs, and also for non-mythical things such as a part of the sky."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "blain-c03",
      "source_id": "blain-s1",
      "quote": "Bláinn gilt als Ableitung von altnordisch blár „blau, dunkel, schwarz“. Man übersetzt somit die mythischen Namen als „der Dunkel-Farbige“, „Blau-Leiche“ oder „der Blauende.“",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bláinn dianggap turunan dari Nordik Kuno blár, \"biru, gelap, hitam\", sehingga diterjemahkan \"yang berwarna gelap\", \"mayat biru\", atau \"yang membiru\".",
        "en": "Bláinn is held to derive from Old Norse blár 'blue, dark, black', so the names are rendered 'the dark-coloured one', 'blue corpse' or 'the bluing one'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "blain-c04",
      "source_id": "blain-s1",
      "quote": "„Da schritten alle Rater zum Richterstuhl, die heiligsten Götter, und beratschlagten, wer das Volk der Zwerge erschaffen sollte aus Brimirs Blut und aus Blainns Knochen.“",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Völuspá 9: para dewa melangkah ke kursi pengadilan dan bermusyawarah siapa yang akan menciptakan bangsa kurcaci dari darah Brimir dan tulang Blainn; istilah ini dalam puisi Edda hanya muncul di Völuspá.",
        "en": "Völuspá 9: the gods strode to their judgement seats and deliberated who should create the race of dwarfs from Brimir's blood and Blainn's bones; in Eddic poetry the term occurs only in Völuspá."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "blain-c05",
      "source_id": "blain-s1",
      "quote": "Die Wendung „aus Brimirs Blut und Blainns Knochen“ scheint darauf anzuspielen, dass die Götter das Meer und die Berge aus Ymirs Blut und Knochen schufen, wie zwei andere Lieder der Lieder-Edda überliefern, das Grimnismál und das Vafþrúðnismál.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ungkapan \"dari darah Brimir dan tulang Blainn\" tampaknya merujuk penciptaan laut dan gunung dari darah dan tulang Ymir oleh para dewa, seperti dikisahkan Grimnismál dan Vafþrúðnismál.",
        "en": "The phrase 'from Brimir's blood and Blainn's bones' seems to allude to the gods making the sea and mountains from Ymir's blood and bones, as Grimnismál and Vafþrúðnismál relate."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "blain-c06",
      "source_id": "blain-s1",
      "quote": "Man mutmaßt, dass Bláinn als Umschreibung für den Riesen gebraucht werden konnte, weil nach den beiden Liedern der Himmel aus seinem Schädel erschaffen wurde – und damit auch das Blau des Himmels.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Diduga Bláinn bisa dipakai sebagai sebutan bagi sang raksasa karena menurut kedua puisi itu langit, beserta warna birunya, diciptakan dari tengkoraknya.",
        "en": "It is surmised that Bláinn could denote the giant because, per both poems, the sky, and thus its blue, was made from his skull."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "blain-c07",
      "source_id": "blain-s1",
      "quote": "In den Þulur wird Bláinn als Heiti für Zwerg angeführt. Das heißt, ein Dichter konnte Bláinn als alternatives Wort für Zwerg verwenden.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Þulur, Bláinn tercantum sebagai heiti untuk kurcaci; artinya penyair bisa memakai Bláinn sebagai kata lain untuk kurcaci.",
        "en": "In the Þulur Bláinn is listed as a heiti for dwarf; a poet could use Bláinn as another word for dwarf."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "blain-c08",
      "source_id": "blain-s2",
      "quote": "In Norse mythology, Ymir (/ˈiːmɪər/), also called Aurgelmir, Brimir, or Bláinn, is the ancestor of all jötnar.",

        "en": "The English article says Ymir in Norse mythology, also called Aurgelmir, Brimir or Bláinn, is the ancestor of all jötnar."

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Buschgroßmutter (\"nenek semak\") adalah roh hutan perempuan tua dalam cerita rakyat Jerman, ibu dan pemimpin para Moosfräulein, yang membalas kebaikan dengan hadiah dan ejekan dengan penyakit.",
    "en": "The Buschgroßmutter ('shrub grandmother') is an aged female forest spirit of German folklore, mother and leader of the Moosfräulein, who rewards kindness with gifts and mockery with sickness.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "buschgro-mutter-c01",
      "buschgro-mutter-c02",
      "buschgro-mutter-c04",
      "buschgro-mutter-c07"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Buschgroßmutter adalah makhluk legenda Jerman yang terutama dikenal di Thüringen, Sachsen, Silesia, dan Bohemia, dengan nama daerah seperti Pusch-Grohla dan Buschmutter di Silesia serta Buschweibchen di Bohemia. Ia roh hutan yang tinggal di hutan terdalam dan hanya menampakkan diri kepada manusia sekali dalam seratus tahun: kecil, bungkuk, keriput, bermata melotot, berambut panjang seputih salju tetapi kusut dan berkutu, dengan tongkat bengkok dan kaki berlumut.",
      "en": "The Buschgroßmutter is a creature of German legend known chiefly in Thuringia, Saxony, Silesia and Bohemia, under regional names such as Pusch-Grohla and Buschmutter in Silesia and Buschweibchen in Bohemia. She is a forest spirit dwelling in the deepest woods who shows herself to people only once in a hundred years: small, stooped, wrinkled and staring, with long snow-white but tangled, lousy hair, a gnarled stick and moss-grown feet.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "buschgro-mutter-c01",
        "buschgro-mutter-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Sifatnya mendua. Ia meminta orang menyisir atau mencari kutu di rambutnya, dan yang memenuhinya diberi gulungan benang yang tak habis atau daun yang kelak menjadi emas, meski kepalanya sedingin es. Siapa yang mengejeknya akan dihembus napasnya dan jatuh sakit, biasanya ruam kulit. Kabut yang naik dari pegunungan di musim semi dan gugur dianggap tanda ia sedang memasak.",
      "en": "Her nature is two-sided. She asks people to comb or delouse her hair, and those who do are given an endless clew of yarn or leaves that later turn to gold, though her head is as cold as ice. Whoever mocks her is breathed on and falls ill, usually with a rash. Fog rising from the mountains in spring and autumn is taken as a sign that she is cooking.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "buschgro-mutter-c03",
        "buschgro-mutter-c04",
        "buschgro-mutter-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, di tepi Saale ia dianggap nyonya dan ibu para Moosweiblein (Moosfräulein), yang berkeliling negeri bersamanya dengan kereta kecil pada malam-malam suci; ia juga dipakai untuk menakut-nakuti anak dan disamakan dengan Frau Holle atau Perchta. Para mitolog abad ke-19 menganggapnya ratu orang lumut, tetapi kini ia lebih dipandang sebagai setan hutan yang sederhana.",
      "en": "According to the German article, by the Saale she is held to be the mistress and mother of the Moosweiblein (Moosfräulein), who roam the land with her in a little cart during the holy nights; she is also used to frighten children and equated with Frau Holle or Perchta. Nineteenth-century mythologists saw her as queen of the moss people, but she is now rather viewed as a primitive forest demon.",

        "buschgro-mutter-c07",

        "en": "The Buschgroßmutter ('shrub grandmother') is a legendary creature of German folklore, especially in Thuringia, Saxony, Silesia and Bohemia, called Pusch-Grohla and Buschmutter in Silesia and Buschweibchen in Bohemia."

      "quote": "The Buschgroßmutter is a forest spirit living in the deepest woods which shows herself to humans only once every hundred years.       "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Buschgroßmutter adalah roh hutan di hutan terdalam yang menampakkan diri hanya sekali tiap seratus tahun; ia sangat tua, kecil, bungkuk, keriput, bermata melotot, berambut panjang seputih salju tetapi kusut dan berkutu.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Watak Buschgroßmutter mendua antara baik dan jahat; ia meminta orang menyisir atau mencari kutu di rambutnya, dan yang memenuhinya diberi gulungan benang tak habis atau daun hijau atau kuning yang kelak menjadi emas bila tak dibuang.",

      "source_id": "buschgro-mutter-s1",
      "quote": "It is but very difficult to clean and tidy the Buschweibchen's hair because her head is as cold as ice, thus leading to a (temporary) freezing of the helper's hands.       "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Merapikan rambutnya sangat sulit karena kepalanya sedingin es sehingga tangan penolong membeku sementara; bila diejek, Buschgroßmutter membalas dengan menghembuskan napas pada pengejek, yang lalu sakit, biasanya ruam kulit.",
        "en": "Tidying her hair is very hard since her head is ice-cold, briefly freezing the helper's hands; when sneered at, the Buschgroßmutter takes revenge by breathing on the mocker, who falls ill, usually with a rash."

      "id": "buschgro-mutter-c06",
      "source_id": "buschgro-mutter-s1",
      "quote": "Normally the Buschgroßmutter is a solitary figure, but occasionally she is said to be the leader of a horde of Moosfräulein (\"moss ladies\"), female forest spirits which are said to be her daughters.       "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Biasanya Buschgroßmutter penyendiri, tetapi kadang dikatakan memimpin rombongan Moosfräulein, roh hutan perempuan yang disebut putri-putrinya, dan bersama mereka berkeliling negeri pada malam-malam suci.",
        "en": "The Buschgroßmutter is usually solitary, but at times said to lead a band of Moosfräulein, female forest spirits called her daughters, roaming the land with them in the holy nights."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buschgro-mutter-c07",
      "source_id": "buschgro-mutter-s2",
      "quote": "Als Herrin der Moosweiblein wird an der Saale die Buschgroßmutter bezeichnet. Die Buschgroßmutter ist genau genommen die Mutter der Moosweiblein (hier: Moosfräuleins), mit welchen sie im Lande umherzieht, meist in einem kleinen Rollwagen. Sie hat wirres Haar und einen starren Blick. Die Buschgroßmutter ist auch ein Kinderschreck. Sie entspricht der andernorts verbreiteten Frau Holle oder Perchta.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di tepi Saale, Buschgroßmutter disebut nyonya para Moosweiblein; tepatnya ia ibu para Moosweiblein (Moosfräuleins) yang berkeliling negeri bersamanya dengan kereta kecil; rambutnya kusut, tatapannya kaku, ia juga penakut anak, dan sepadan dengan Frau Holle atau Perchta.",
        "en": "By the Saale the Buschgroßmutter is called mistress of the Moosweiblein; strictly she is their mother (Moosfräuleins), roaming the land with them, usually in a little cart; with tangled hair and a fixed stare, she is also a bogey for children and corresponds to Frau Holle or Perchta."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buschgro-mutter-c08",
      "source_id": "buschgro-mutter-s1",
      "quote": "In the 19th century, mythologists such as Jacob Grimm or Wilhelm Mannhardt declared the Buschgroßmutter to be the leader or even the queen of the moss people, similar to the elf queen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada abad ke-19 para mitolog seperti Jacob Grimm dan Wilhelm Mannhardt menyatakan Buschgroßmutter pemimpin atau bahkan ratu orang lumut, seperti ratu elf.",
        "en": "In the 19th century mythologists like Jacob Grimm and Wilhelm Mannhardt made the Buschgroßmutter leader or even queen of the moss people, like an elf queen."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buschgro-mutter-c09",
      "source_id": "buschgro-mutter-s1",
      "quote": "In traditional folktales of the German-speaking Transylvanian Saxons in Transylvania, today part of Romania, there is a similar being called Baschgrîs (\"shrub grandmother\"), Baschmôter (\"shrub mother\") or Baschäinjel (\"shrub angel\") in Transylvanian Saxon dialect.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Saxon Transilvania (Transylvanian Saxons) berbahasa Jerman di Transilvania, kini bagian Rumania, terdapat makhluk serupa bernama Baschgrîs, Baschmôter, atau Baschäinjel.",
        "en": "In folktales of the German-speaking Transylvanian Saxons, in today's Romania, there is a similar being called Baschgrîs, Baschmôter or Baschäinjel."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buschgro-mutter-c10",
      "source_id": "buschgro-mutter-s2",
      "quote": "Während sie von den Mythologen des 19. Jahrhunderts als Königin der Moosleute betrachtet wurde, wird sie doch eher als primitiver Walddämon gesehen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meski para mitolog abad ke-19 menganggapnya ratu orang lumut (Moosleute), ia lebih dipandang sebagai setan hutan primitif.",
        "en": "Though 19th-century mythologists regarded her as queen of the moss people (Moosleute), she is rather seen as a primitive forest demon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "buschgro-mutter-c11",
      "source_id": "buschgro-mutter-s1",
      "quote": "All three names are used to scare children and denote a female wood sprite with shaggy fluttering hair, large fiery eyes and very big teeth.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ketiga nama itu dipakai untuk menakuti anak-anak dan menunjuk roh hutan perempuan berambut kusut berkibar, bermata besar menyala, dan bergigi sangat besar.",
        "en": "All three names serve to scare children and denote a female wood sprite with shaggy, fluttering hair, large fiery eyes and very big teeth."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",

  ]
}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Dvalinn adalah salah satu dari empat rusa dalam mitologi Nordik yang berlarian di dahan pohon dunia Yggdrasil dan memakan dedaunannya, berbeda dari kurcaci bernama sama.",
    "en": "Dvalinn is one of the four stags of Norse mythology that run about the branches of the world tree Yggdrasil and graze its foliage, distinct from the dwarf of the same name.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dvalinn (juga Dwalin atau Dvalar) adalah salah satu dari empat rusa dalam mitologi Nordik, bersama Dáinn, Duneyrr, dan Duraþrór, yang termasuk hewan-hewan di pohon dunia Yggdrasil. Menurut Gylfaginning dalam Edda Muda, keempat rusa itu berlarian di dahan Yggdrasil dan merumput di dedaunannya; mereka juga disebut dalam Grímnismál di Edda Tua.",
      "en": "Dvalinn (also Dwalin or Dvalar) is one of the four stags of Norse mythology, together with Dáinn, Duneyrr and Duraþrór, counted among the animals of the world tree Yggdrasil. According to Gylfaginning in the Younger Edda, the four run about Yggdrasil's branches and graze its leaves; they are also named in Grímnismál in the Elder Edda.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "dvalinn-q118525447-c01",
        "dvalinn-q118525447-c02",
        "dvalinn-q118525447-c03"
      ]

    {
      "id": "Rusa-rusa ini termasuk dalam gambaran banyak hewan yang menghuni Yggdrasil: elang Vidofnir di puncaknya, ular Nidhogg yang melilit akarnya, dan tupai Ratatosk yang berlari naik-turun membawa kata-kata kebencian. Penulis Vera Henriksen berpendapat bahwa hewan-hewan yang ikut merusak pohon dunia ini adalah \"awal kesengsaraan\" yang berakhir dengan Ragnarok.",
      "en": "The stags form part of the description of the many creatures dwelling in Yggdrasil: the eagle Vidofnir at its top, the serpent Nidhogg coiled round its root and the squirrel Ratatosk running up and down carrying hateful words.       "quote": "Dain, Dwalin (auch Dvalin oder Dvalar), Duneyr und Durathror sind vier Hirsche in der nordischen Mythologie, die zu den Tieren am Weltenbaum Yggdrasil gehören.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dain, Dwalin (juga Dvalin atau Dvalar), Duneyr, dan Durathror adalah empat rusa dalam mitologi Nordik (nordischen) yang termasuk hewan-hewan di pohon dunia Yggdrasil.",
        "en": "Dain, Dwalin (also Dvalin or Dvalar), Duneyr and Durathror are four stags in Norse (nordischen) mythology belonging to the animals of the world tree Yggdrasil."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dvalinn-q118525447-c02",
      "source_id": "dvalinn-q118525447-s2",
      "quote": "Dåin, Dvalin, Dunøyr og Duratro (norrønt: Dáinn, Dvalinn, Duneyrr og Duraþrór) er fire hjortedyr som ifølge Gylvaginning i Den yngre Edda løper omkring på greinene til verdenstreet Yggdrasil og beiter i løvverket.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dåin, Dvalin, Dunøyr, dan Duratro (Nordik Kuno: Dáinn, Dvalinn, Duneyrr, Duraþrór) adalah empat rusa yang menurut Gylfaginning (Gylvaginning) dalam Edda Muda berlari di dahan pohon dunia Yggdrasil dan merumput di dedaunannya.",
        "en": "Dåin, Dvalin, Dunøyr and Duratro (Old Norse Dáinn, Dvalinn, Duneyrr, Duraþrór) are four deer that, per Gylfaginning (Gylvaginning) in the Younger Edda, run about Yggdrasil's branches and graze its foliage."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dvalinn-q118525447-c03",
      "source_id": "dvalinn-q118525447-s2",
      "quote": "De fire hjortene omtales også i Grimnesmål i Den eldre Edda",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Keempat rusa itu juga disebut dalam Grímnismál (Grimnesmål) di Edda Tua.",
        "en": "The four stags are also mentioned in Grímnismál (Grimnesmål) in the Elder Edda."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dvalinn-q118525447-c04",
      "source_id": "dvalinn-q118525447-s2",
      "quote": "Hjortene inngår i beskrivelsen av mange dyr som har tilhold i Yggdrasil. I toppen sitter ørnen Vidofnir med hauken Verfolne mellom øynene. Rundt rota på treet snor slangen Nidhogg seg, mens ekornet Ratatosk løper opp og ned og bringer hatefulle ord mellom dem.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Rusa-rusa itu bagian dari gambaran banyak hewan di Yggdrasil: elang Vidofnir di puncak dengan elang kecil Verfolne di antara matanya, ular Nidhogg melilit akarnya, dan tupai Ratatosk berlari naik-turun membawa kata-kata kebencian.",
        "en": "The stags belong to the description of the many animals in Yggdrasil: the eagle Vidofnir at the top with the hawk Verfolne between its eyes, the serpent Nidhogg coiled round its root and the squirrel Ratatosk running up and down bearing hateful words."

      "source_id": "dvalinn-q118525447-s2",
      "quote": "Forfatteren Vera Henriksen har hevdet at dyrene som bidrar til å ødelegge verdenstreet er «begynnelsen på elendigheten» som ender i Ragnarok.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Elder Mother adalah makhluk penjaga pohon elder dalam cerita rakyat Inggris dan Skandinavia, yang harus dimintai izin sebelum kayu elder diambil.",
    "en": "The Elder Mother is a being that guards the elder tree in English and Scandinavian folklore, whose leave must be asked before elder wood is taken.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "elder-mother-c01",
      "elder-mother-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Elder Mother, yang dikenal di Denmark sebagai Hyldemoer dan di Lincolnshire sebagai Old Lady atau Old Girl, adalah makhluk penjaga pohon elder dalam cerita rakyat Inggris dan Skandinavia. Hingga belum lama ini, di berbagai bagian Inggris dan Skandinavia orang mengatakan bahwa sebelum mengambil kayu elder seseorang harus meminta izin kepadanya, atau nasib sial akan menimpa si penebang.",
      "en": "The Elder Mother, called Hyldemoer in Denmark and the Old Lady or Old Girl in Lincolnshire, is a being that guards the elder tree in English and Scandinavian folklore. Until recently, people in parts of England and Scandinavia said that before taking elder wood one had to ask her permission, or misfortune would strike the woodcutter.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "elder-mother-c01",
        "elder-mother-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Di Denmark, Hyldemoer atau Hyldequinde (\"perempuan elder\") adalah roh mirip nimfa hutan atau dryad yang tinggal di pohon elder, dan konon menghantui orang yang membangun dengan kayu elder tanpa meminta izin. Ada dugaan bahwa kepercayaan ini berasal dari Skandinavia dan dibawa ke Inggris oleh bangsa Viking pada masa Danelaw.",
      "en": "Danish tradition knows her as the Hyldemoer or Hyldequinde ('elder woman'), a dryad-like spirit dwelling in the elder tree, who is said to plague anyone building with its wood without first asking leave. It has been suggested that the belief came from Scandinavia and was brought to England by the Vikings in the time of the Danelaw.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "elder-mother-c06",
        "elder-mother-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam kisah-kisah kemudian, Elder Mother bergeser menjadi penyihir. Di Somerset seorang petani melihatnya dalam wujud pohon elder sedang memerah sapinya; tembakan peluru peraknya meleset, tetapi nenek tua di rumah melempar bara ke pohon itu hingga terbakar dan sang penyihir mati. Kisah lain menyebut penyihir pohon elder menyelamatkan Inggris dengan mengubah seorang raja dan para ksatrianya menjadi Batu Rollright, lalu ia sendiri menjadi pohon elder.",
      "en": "In later tales the Elder Mother shifts into a witch. In Somerset a farmer saw her, in the form of an elder tree, milking his cow; his silver bullet missed, but the old grandmother of the house threw burning coals at the tree so that it burned and the witch died. Another tale has the elder-tree witch saving England by turning a king and his knights into the Rollright Stones, then turning herself into an elder tree.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "elder-mother-c03",
        "elder-mother-c04",
        "elder-mother-c05",
        "elder-mother-c08"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,

    "id": "Menurut legenda, Batu Rollright di perbatasan Oxfordshire dan Warwickshire adalah seorang raja dan para ksatrianya yang diubah menjadi batu oleh penyihir pohon elder.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "elder-mother-c01",
      "source_id": "elder-mother-s1",
      "quote": "The Elder Mother is an elder-guarding being in English and Scandinavian folklore known by a variety of names, such as the Danish Hyldemoer (\"Elder-Mother\") and the Lincolnshire names Old Lady and Old Girl.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Elder Mother adalah makhluk penjaga pohon elder dalam cerita rakyat Inggris (English) dan Skandinavia (Scandinavian) dengan banyak nama, seperti Hyldemoer dari Denmark (Danish) dan Old Lady atau Old Girl di Lincolnshire.",
        "en": "The Elder Mother is an elder-guarding being in English and Scandinavian folklore with many names, such as the Danish Hyldemoer and the Lincolnshire Old Lady and Old Girl."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elder-mother-c02",
      "source_id": "elder-mother-s1",
      "quote": "The Elder Mother is thought to be the guardian of the elder trees, and it was said, until recent times in various parts of England and Scandinavia that to take wood from the elder tree one would have to ask the Elder Mother first, or else ill luck would befall the woodsman.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Elder Mother dianggap penjaga pohon elder; hingga belum lama ini di berbagai bagian Inggris (England) dan Skandinavia (Scandinavia) dikatakan bahwa untuk mengambil kayu elder orang harus meminta izinnya dulu, atau penebang akan tertimpa sial.",
        "en": "The Elder Mother is held to guard elder trees; until recently in parts of England and Scandinavia it was said one must first ask her before taking elder wood, or ill luck would befall the woodsman."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elder-mother-c03",
      "source_id": "elder-mother-s1",
      "quote": "Another tale, from Somerset, casts the Elder Mother as the villain, a witch that a farmer sees as an elder milking his cow. The farmer shoots at the witch with a silver bullet but misses and is chased back into the farmhouse. The old granny, however, picks up the burning coal from the fire with a shovel and throws it at the elder tree, burning cinders, and thus the witch is dead.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah dari Somerset menjadikan Elder Mother penjahat, penyihir yang dilihat petani dalam wujud pohon elder sedang memerah sapinya; tembakan peluru peraknya meleset dan ia dikejar ke rumah, tetapi nenek tua melempar bara ke pohon elder dan penyihir itu mati.",
        "en": "A Somerset tale makes the Elder Mother a villain, a witch a farmer sees as an elder milking his cow; his silver bullet misses and he is chased indoors, but the old granny throws burning coal at the elder and the witch dies."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elder-mother-c04",
      "source_id": "elder-mother-s2",
      "quote": "Yn lettere ferhalen ferskoot de persepsje fan 'e Flearemem, grif ûnder kristlike ynfloed, fan in beskermfrouwe fan flearebeammen nei in hekse dy't al of net boasaardige bedoelings hat.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kisah-kisah kemudian, kemungkinan karena pengaruh Kristen, gambaran Flearemem (Elder Mother) bergeser dari pelindung pohon elder menjadi penyihir yang bisa bermaksud jahat atau tidak.",
        "en": "In later tales, probably under Christian influence, the image of the Flearemem (Elder Mother) shifted from protector of elder trees to a witch who may or may not mean harm."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elder-mother-c05",
      "source_id": "elder-mother-s1",
      "quote": "And thus the king and his knights were turned to stone and the witch turned herself into an elder tree.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Demikianlah raja dan para ksatrianya diubah menjadi batu, dan penyihir itu mengubah dirinya menjadi pohon elder.",
        "en": "Thus the king and his knights were turned to stone and the witch turned herself into an elder tree."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elder-mother-c06",
      "source_id": "elder-mother-s1",
      "quote": "In Denmark, the Hyldemoer (“Elder-mother”) or Hyldequinde (“Elder-woman”) is a spirit like a wood-nymph or dryad that lives in the elder tree. The spirit is said to haunt or torment people who build from elder wood unless they ask permission first.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Denmark (Denmark), Hyldemoer atau Hyldequinde (\"perempuan elder\") adalah roh mirip nimfa hutan atau dryad yang tinggal di pohon elder, dan konon menghantui atau menyiksa orang yang membangun dengan kayu elder tanpa meminta izin.",
        "en": "In Denmark the Hyldemoer or Hyldequinde ('elder woman') is a spirit like a wood nymph or dryad living in the elder tree, said to haunt or torment those who build with elder wood without asking permission."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elder-mother-c07",
      "source_id": "elder-mother-s2",
      "quote": "It soe skoan kinne dat it leauwen yn 'e Flearemem syn oarsprong fynt yn Skandinaavje en yn 'e Iere Midsiuwen troch de Wytsingen yn 'e tiid fan 'e Danelaw yn Ingelân brocht is.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mungkin saja kepercayaan akan Flearemem berasal dari Skandinavia (Skandinaavje) dan dibawa ke Inggris (Ingelân) oleh bangsa Viking (Wytsingen) pada awal Abad Pertengahan, masa Danelaw.",
        "en": "The belief in the Flearemem may well have originated in Scandinavia (Skandinaavje) and been brought to England (Ingelân) by the Vikings (Wytsingen) in the early Middle Ages, in the Danelaw era."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elder-mother-c08",
      "source_id": "elder-mother-s2",
      "quote": "Yn ien sa'n ferhaal feroaret de Flearmem sa'n kening en syn mannen yn 'e Rollright Stones, dy't op 'e grins fan 'e Ingelske greefskippen Oxfordshire en Warwickshire steane, wylst se harsels yn in flearebeam feroaret.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam satu kisah, Flearemem mengubah seorang raja dan anak buahnya menjadi Batu Rollright (Rollright Stones) di perbatasan Oxfordshire dan Warwickshire, sementara ia sendiri berubah menjadi pohon elder.",
        "en": "In one such tale the Flearemem turns a king and his men into the Rollright Stones on the Oxfordshire–Warwickshire border, while turning herself into an elder tree."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

    "id": "Erawan adalah bentuk Thailand dari gajah mitis Airavata: gajah putih raksasa berkepala banyak yang menjadi tunggangan Dewa Indra di surga Tavatimsa.",
    "en": "Erawan is the Thai form of the mythical elephant Airavata: a giant, many-headed white elephant that serves as the mount of the god Indra in the Tavatimsa heaven.",
    "claim_ids": [

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Erawan adalah bentuk Thailand dari gajah mitis Airavata. Ia gajah raksasa sebesar gunung, biasanya berkepala tiga dan kadang hingga 33, masing-masing dengan tujuh gading. Ia tunggangan Indra, dewa hujan dan guntur, penguasa surga Tavatimsa, tempat tinggal 33 dewa.",
      "en": "Erawan is the Thai form of the mythical elephant Airavata. He is a giant elephant as big as a mountain, usually with three heads and sometimes as many as 33, each bearing seven tusks. He is the mount of Indra, god of rain and thunder and ruler of the Tavatimsa heaven, home of 33 gods.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "erawan-c01",
        "erawan-c02",
        "erawan-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Karya Thailand abad ke-14, Traiphum Phra Ruang, menggambarkan Erawan bukan sebagai hewan, sebab di surga itu tidak ada hewan, melainkan devatā bernama \"Devatā Erawan\". Setiap kali Indra ingin berkuda untuk bersenang-senang, Devatā Erawan menjelma menjadi gajah putih yang sangat besar dengan 33 kepala; kepala di tengah, Sudassana, menjadi singgasana Indra.",
      "en": "The 14th-century Thai work Traiphum Phra Ruang describes Erawan not as an animal, since there are no animals in that heaven, but as a devatā called 'Devatā Erawan'.       "claim_ids": [

    {
      "id": "Erawan adalah motif keagamaan penting di Thailand dan sering dipakai sebagai ornamen, misalnya pada tanda kehormatan Orde Chula Chom Klao dan pada berbagai monumen; nama I-rapote juga dipakai di Thailand.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "erawan-c01",
      "source_id": "erawan-s1",
      "quote": "Erawan (in Thai: เอราวัณ) ist die thailändische Form des mythischen Elefanten Airavata.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Erawan (Thai: เอราวัณ) adalah bentuk Thailand (thailändische) dari gajah mitis Airavata.",
        "en": "Erawan (Thai: เอราวัณ) is the Thai (thailändische) form of the mythical elephant Airavata."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "erawan-c02",
      "source_id": "erawan-s1",
      "quote": "Er ist ein riesenhafter Elefant, groß wie ein Berg. Er hat meistens drei, manchmal sogar 33 Köpfe, jeder Kopf ist mit sieben Stoßzähnen ausgestattet.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Erawan adalah gajah raksasa sebesar gunung, biasanya berkepala tiga, kadang sampai 33, tiap kepala bergading tujuh.",
        "en": "Erawan is a giant elephant as big as a mountain, usually with three heads, sometimes 33, each with seven tusks."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "erawan-c03",
      "source_id": "erawan-s1",
      "quote": "Er ist das Reittier des hinduistischen Gottes des Regens und des Donners Indra, dem Herrscher über den Tavatimsa-Himmel, der Wohnstatt von 33 Göttern vedischen Ursprungs.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Erawan adalah tunggangan Indra, dewa Hindu (hinduistischen) hujan dan guntur, penguasa surga Tavatimsa, kediaman 33 dewa berasal-usul Weda.",
        "en": "Erawan is the mount of Indra, the Hindu god of rain and thunder, ruler of the Tavatimsa heaven, home of 33 gods of Vedic origin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "erawan-c04",
      "source_id": "erawan-s1",
      "quote": "In der Stadt gibt es einen Elefanten mit Namen Erawan. Allerdings ist dieser Elefant kein Tier, denn in diesem Himmel gibt es keine Tiere, er ist ausschließlich von Devatā bewohnt. So gibt es dort einen Elefanten mit Namen „Devatā Erawan“.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Traiphum Phra Ruang (abad ke-14), di kota Indra ada gajah bernama Erawan, tetapi ia bukan hewan karena di surga itu tidak ada hewan, hanya devatā; maka ia disebut \"Devatā Erawan\".",
        "en": "Per the Traiphum Phra Ruang (14th century), in Indra's city there is an elephant named Erawan, but it is no animal, since that heaven holds no animals, only devatā; hence it is called 'Devatā Erawan'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "erawan-c05",
      "source_id": "erawan-s1",
      "quote": "Wann immer König Indra zum Vergnügen ausreiten möchte, und er möchte dabei auf einem Elefanten reiten, verwandelt sich „Devatā Erawan“ in einen sehr großen weißen Elefanten. Dieser Elefant ist 1.200.000 Wa hoch und hat 33 Köpfe.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setiap kali Raja Indra ingin berkuda untuk bersenang-senang dengan menunggang gajah, Devatā Erawan menjelma menjadi gajah putih yang sangat besar, setinggi 1.200.000 wa dan berkepala 33.",
        "en": "Whenever King Indra wishes to ride out for pleasure on an elephant, Devatā Erawan becomes a huge white elephant 1,200,000 wa tall with 33 heads."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "erawan-c06",
      "source_id": "erawan-s2",
      "quote": "Deze reusachtige witte olifant is het rijdier van Indra en bevindt zich in de hemel Tavatimsa. Het dier wordt afgebeeld met drie of meer driekoppige olifantskoppen. Ook de naam \"I-rapote\" wordt in Thailand gebruikt.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gajah putih raksasa ini tunggangan Indra dan berada di surga Tavatimsa; ia digambarkan dengan tiga kepala gajah atau lebih, dan nama I-rapote juga dipakai di Thailand.",
        "en": "This giant white elephant is Indra's mount and dwells in the Tavatimsa heaven; it is shown with three or more elephant heads, and the name I-rapote is also used in Thailand."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "erawan-c07",
      "source_id": "erawan-s2",
      "quote": "De Erawan is een belangrijk religieus motief en de olifant wordt vaak als ornament gebruikt. Men ziet hem op Thaise onderscheidingen als de Orde van Chula Chom Klao waarin de erawan een centrale plaats in de keten kreeg en op monumenten.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Erawan adalah motif keagamaan penting dan sering dipakai sebagai ornamen, misalnya pada tanda kehormatan Thailand (Thaise) Orde Chula Chom Klao dan pada monumen.",
        "en": "Erawan is an important religious motif often used as ornament, as on Thai honours like the Order of Chula Chom Klao and on monuments."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "erawan-c08",
      "source_id": "erawan-s1",
      "quote": "In jedem Stoßzahn gibt es sieben Teiche, in jedem Teich sieben Lotuspflanzen; jede Lotuspflanze hat sieben Blüten, eine jede mit sieben Blütenblättern.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam setiap gading terdapat tujuh kolam, dalam setiap kolam tujuh tanaman teratai, setiap teratai bertujuh bunga, masing-masing bertujuh kelopak.",
        "en": "In each tusk there are seven ponds, in each pond seven lotus plants; each lotus has seven flowers, each with seven petals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "erawan-c09",
      "source_id": "erawan-s1",
      "quote": "Die Köpfe zur Mitte hin werden immer größer, der Kopf im Zentrum heißt Sudassana. Er ist der Thron Indras und hat einen Umfang von 24.000 Wa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kepala-kepala makin besar ke arah tengah; kepala di tengah bernama Sudassana, singgasana Indra, dengan keliling 24.000 wa.",
        "en": "The heads grow ever larger toward the middle; the central head is called Sudassana, Indra's throne, with a circumference of 24,000 wa."
      }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "falhofnir-c01",
      "source_id": "falhofnir-s1",
      "quote": "In Norse mythology, Falhófnir (Old Norse: [ˈfɑlˌhoːvnez̠] ) is a horse listed in both Grímnismál and Gylfaginning among the steeds ridden by the gods each day when they go to make judgements at Yggdrasil. However, in both stanzas Falhófnir is not assigned to any specific deity.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Nordik, Falhófnir adalah kuda yang tercantum dalam Grímnismál dan Gylfaginning di antara tunggangan para dewa yang setiap hari pergi memberi keputusan di Yggdrasil, tanpa dikaitkan dengan dewa tertentu.",
        "en": "In Norse mythology Falhófnir is a horse listed in Grímnismál and Gylfaginning among the mounts the gods ride daily to give judgements at Yggdrasil, not tied to any specific god."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "falhofnir-c02",
      "source_id": "falhofnir-s2",
      "quote": "Falhófnir (norrønt for \"behåret hov\" eller \"skjult hov\") er en hest i nordisk mytologi, som bliver nævnt i både Grímnismál og Gylfaginning som en af de hingste, som aserne rider på hver dag, når drager til Yggdrasil for at dømme ting. I ingen af stanzaerne bliver ejerskabet af hesten tilskrevet til en bestemt gud, som det er tilfældet med bl.a. Sleipner og Frejfaxe.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Denmark mengartikan nama Falhófnir sebagai \"kuku berbulu\" atau \"kuku tersembunyi\"; ia salah satu kuda jantan yang ditunggangi para Æsir (aserne) ke Yggdrasil, dan tak satu bait pun menyebut pemiliknya, berbeda dengan Sleipnir (Sleipner) dan Freyfaxi (Frejfaxe).",
        "en": "The Danish article glosses Falhófnir as 'hairy hoof' or 'hidden hoof'; it is one of the stallions the Æsir (aserne) ride to Yggdrasil, and no stanza names its owner, unlike Sleipnir (Sleipner) and Freyfaxi (Frejfaxe)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "falhofnir-c03",
      "source_id": "falhofnir-s3",
      "quote": "These are the names of the Æsir's steeds: Sleipnir is best, which Odin has; he has eight feet. The second is Gladr, the third Gyllir, the fourth Glenr, the fifth Skeidbrimir, the sixth Silfrintoppr, the seventh Sinir, the eighth Gisl, the ninth Falhófnir, the tenth Gulltoppr, the eleventh Léttfeti.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gylfaginning (terjemahan Brodeur) mendaftar kuda para Æsir: Sleipnir yang terbaik milik Odin dan berkaki delapan, lalu Gladr, Gyllir, Glenr, Skeidbrimir, Silfrintoppr, Sinir, Gisl, Falhófnir, Gulltoppr, dan Léttfeti.",
        "en": "Gylfaginning (Brodeur's translation) lists the Æsir's steeds: Sleipnir, Odin's eight-footed best, then Gladr, Gyllir, Glenr, Skeidbrimir, Silfrintoppr, Sinir, Gisl, Falhófnir, Gulltoppr and Léttfeti."
      }
    }
  ],

      ]

  ],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Fänggen adalah roh hutan atau \"perempuan liar\" dalam cerita rakyat Jerman di wilayah Alpen, umumnya raksasa perempuan berbulu yang hidupnya terikat pada hutan tertentu.",
    "en": "The Fänggen are wood sprites or 'wild women' of German folklore in the Alpine region, usually hairy giantesses whose lives are bound to a particular forest.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "fanggen-c01",
      "fanggen-c02",
      "fanggen-c09"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Fängge (jamak Fänggen) adalah roh hutan atau sejenis perempuan liar dalam cerita rakyat Jerman, dikenal di wilayah Alpen berbahasa Jerman hingga Oberpfalz di Bayern, serta di Liechtenstein, Tirol, dan Swiss. Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, tergantung pengarang, masa, dan tempat, mereka digambarkan sebagai setan hutan raksasa, sosok penyihir, atau peri hutan yang terikat pada pohon.",
      "en": "The Fängge (plural Fänggen) is a wood sprite or kind of wild woman in German folklore, known in the German-speaking Alpine region as far as the Upper Palatinate in Bavaria, and in Liechtenstein, Tyrol and Switzerland. According to the German article, depending on author, period and place they are pictured as giant forest demons, witch-like figures or tree-bound forest fairies.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "fanggen-c01",
        "fanggen-c03",
        "fanggen-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Fängge umumnya dibayangkan sebagai raksasa perempuan berbulu dan buruk rupa, setinggi pohon, padanan lelaki liar berlumut; namun batas jenis kelaminnya kabur. Johann Nepomuk von Alpenburg (1857) melukiskannya bertubuh sangat besar dan berbulu, mulut terentang dari telinga ke telinga, mata sehitam arang yang kadang menyala, bersuara lelaki, dan berpakaian kulit kucing hutan serta kulit kayu.",
      "en": "The Fängge is usually imagined as a hairy, ugly giantess as tall as a tree, counterpart of the moss-grown wild man; yet the line between sexes is vague. Johann Nepomuk von Alpenburg (1857) describes it as huge and hairy, with a mouth stretching from ear to ear, coal-black eyes that sometimes glow, a man's voice, and clothing of wildcat fur and bark.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "fanggen-c02",
        "fanggen-c05",
        "fanggen-c07",
        "fanggen-c12"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mereka konon menyimpan pengetahuan rahasia, seperti khasiat tersembunyi tumbuhan, yang bisa dikorek setelah mereka dibuat mabuk, dan banyak yang punya nama diri seperti Rauhrinda dan Stutzförcha. Dalam kisah-kisah mereka sering menjadi momok anak-anak; sebuah legenda Tirol menyebut Fangg setinggi pohon yang mengisap anak lelaki ke hidungnya seperti tembakau. Hidup mereka terikat pada hutan tertentu dan akan lenyap bila hutan itu ditebang; dentang lonceng gereja dapat mengusir mereka.",
      "en": "They are said to hold secret knowledge, such as the hidden powers of plants, which can be coaxed out of them once they are drunk, and many bear names such as Rauhrinda and Stutzförcha. In tales they are often reduced to bogeys for children; a Tyrolean legend tells of a tree-tall Fangg that snuffed little boys up her nose like snuff. Their lives are tied to a particular forest and they vanish if it is felled; the ringing of a church bell drives them off.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "fanggen-c08",
        "fanggen-c09",
        "fanggen-c11",
        "fanggen-c14"
      ]
    }
  ],
