  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c01",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s1",
      "quote": "A Fängge (plural Fänggen ) is a wood sprite or a subtype of wild women in German folklore, found in German-speaking parts of the Alpine Region into more northerly non-Alpine parts of Bavaria (Upper Palatinate).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fängge (jamak Fänggen) adalah roh hutan atau sejenis perempuan liar dalam cerita rakyat Jerman (German), dikenal di wilayah Alpen (Alpine) berbahasa Jerman hingga Bayern (Bavaria) utara, Oberpfalz (Upper Palatinate).",
        "en": "A Fängge (plural Fänggen) is a wood sprite or kind of wild woman in German folklore, found in the German-speaking Alpine region and further north in Bavaria (Upper Palatinate)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c02",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s1",
      "quote": "The Fängge has been conceived of strictly as female, a hairy, ugly giantess (counterpart of the moss-grown male giant or wild man). However, the gender demarcation is vague.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fängge dibayangkan khusus perempuan, raksasa perempuan berbulu dan buruk rupa (padanan raksasa lelaki atau lelaki liar berlumut), meski batas jenis kelaminnya kabur.",
        "en": "The Fängge is conceived as strictly female, a hairy, ugly giantess (counterpart of the moss-grown male giant or wild man), though the gender line is vague."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c03",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s1",
      "quote": "Fängge in the broad sense refers to a nature spirit, either gigantic or dwarfish, in Bavaria (Upper Palatinate), Liechtenstein, Tyrol, as well as in Switzerland",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam arti luas, Fängge (juga Wildfanggen) menunjuk roh alam, raksasa maupun kerdil, di Bayern (Bavaria; Upper Palatinate), Liechtenstein, Tirol (Tyrol), dan Swiss (Switzerland).",
        "en": "In the broad sense Fängge (also Wildfanggen) means a nature spirit, giant or dwarfish, in Bavaria (Upper Palatinate), Liechtenstein, Tyrol and Switzerland."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c04",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s1",
      "quote": "The Fankerl (plural Fankerln ) is a type of bearded dwarf with flashy red eyes living on the Pfreimd River according to the lore of the Upper Palatinate, Bavaria, and it is counted as a type of Fängge.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fankerl adalah kurcaci berjanggut bermata merah menyala yang tinggal di Sungai Pfreimd menurut cerita Oberpfalz (Upper Palatinate), Bayern (Bavaria), dan digolongkan sebagai Fängge.",
        "en": "The Fankerl is a bearded dwarf with flashy red eyes on the Pfreimd River in Upper Palatinate lore, Bavaria, counted as a type of Fängge."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c05",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s1",
      "quote": "The Fängge (var. Fangga ) is a giantess as tall as a tree, according to the lore of Tyrol and the Bavarian Alps.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut cerita Tirol (Tyrol) dan Alpen Bayern (Bavarian Alps), Fängge (Fangga) adalah raksasa perempuan setinggi pohon.",
        "en": "Per the lore of Tyrol and the Bavarian Alps, the Fängge (Fangga) is a giantess as tall as a tree."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c06",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s2",
      "quote": "Die Fänggen (Sing.: Fangga; Fanggin), welche auch Fänken, Fenggen und Fanggen genannt werden, sind Wesen und Sagengestalten in Süddeutschland, Österreich und Südtirol. Je nach Autor, Zeit und Ort werden sie entweder als riesenhafte Walddämonen, als Hexengestalten oder auch als baumverbundene Waldfeen beschrieben.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fänggen (tunggal Fangga, Fanggin), juga Fänken, Fenggen, dan Fanggen, adalah makhluk legenda di Jerman selatan (Süddeutschland), Austria (Österreich), dan Tirol Selatan (Südtirol); tergantung pengarang, masa, dan tempat mereka digambarkan sebagai setan hutan raksasa, sosok penyihir, atau peri hutan yang terikat pada pohon.",
        "en": "The Fänggen (singular Fangga, Fanggin), also Fänken, Fenggen and Fanggen, are legendary beings of southern Germany (Süddeutschland), Austria (Österreich) and South Tyrol (Südtirol); by author, time and place they are giant forest demons, witch figures or tree-bound forest fairies."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c07",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s2",
      "quote": "riesengroß, am ganzen Körper behaart, geborstet, das Antlitz verzerrt, der Mund von einem Ohre zum anderen gezogen, das schwarze Haupthaar hängt voll Baumbart",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Johann Nepomuk von Alpenburg (1857) melukiskan Fänggen sangat besar, berbulu sekujur tubuh, wajah berkerut, mulut terentang dari telinga ke telinga, rambut hitam penuh lumut janggut pohon.",
        "en": "Johann Nepomuk von Alpenburg (1857) describes the Fänggen as huge, hairy all over, with a distorted face, a mouth stretched from ear to ear and black hair hung with tree-beard."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c08",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s2",
      "quote": "Es heißt, dass sie über geheimes Wissen verfügen, wie zum Beispiel jenes über die geheimen Kräfte der Pflanzen, das man ihnen entlocken kann, wenn man sie erst betrunken gemacht hat. Auch sollen die meisten über Eigennamen verfügen, wie zum Beispiel Rauhrinda und Stutzförcha. In Sagen werden sie häufig zum Kinderschreck deklassiert oder als dumm dargestellt.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka konon memiliki pengetahuan rahasia, misalnya tentang khasiat tersembunyi tumbuhan, yang bisa dikorek setelah mereka dibuat mabuk; kebanyakan punya nama diri seperti Rauhrinda dan Stutzförcha; dalam legenda mereka sering dijadikan momok anak atau digambarkan bodoh.",
        "en": "They are said to have secret knowledge, such as the hidden powers of plants, which can be coaxed from them once drunk; most have names like Rauhrinda and Stutzförcha; in legends they are often reduced to bogeys for children or shown as stupid."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c09",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s2",
      "quote": "Sie sollen sowohl solitär, als auch in Gruppen auftreten, wie in den oben beschriebenen Sagen, sind dabei aber stets an einen bestimmten Wald mit ihrem Leben gebunden. Würde besagter Wald verschwinden, etwa durch Abholzung, würden auch sie verschwinden.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka muncul sendirian maupun berkelompok, tetapi hidupnya selalu terikat pada hutan tertentu; bila hutan itu hilang, misalnya karena ditebang, mereka pun lenyap.",
        "en": "They appear alone or in groups, but their lives are always bound to a particular forest; if it disappears, say through felling, they disappear too."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c10",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s2",
      "quote": "Neben den riesenhaften Fänggen existieren in manchen Sagen und Erzählungen auch kleine, zwerghafte Fängge. Diese Winzlinge sollen in Felsenklüften und Erdhügeln, die Fänggenlöcher genannt werden, hausen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Selain Fänggen raksasa, dalam beberapa legenda ada pula Fängge kecil seperti kurcaci yang tinggal di celah batu dan gundukan tanah yang disebut Fänggenlöcher.",
        "en": "Besides giant Fänggen, some legends know small dwarf-like Fängge living in rock clefts and mounds called Fänggenlöcher."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c11",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s2",
      "quote": "Ein weiteres Merkmal der Fänggen, das sicherlich erst im Zuge der Christianisierung hinzukam, ist, dass man sie, wie auch andere heidnische Gestalten durch das Läuten einer Kirchenglocke vertreiben kann.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ciri lain yang pasti baru muncul seiring Kristenisasi: Fänggen, seperti sosok pagan lain, dapat diusir dengan dentang lonceng gereja.",
        "en": "Another trait, surely added with Christianisation, is that the Fänggen, like other pagan figures, can be driven off by the ringing of a church bell."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c12",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s2",
      "quote": "Die Augen sind dunkel und nachtschwarz wie Kohlen, glühen aber auch zu Zeiten und sprühen Blitze – die Stimme ist Mannesstimme, rauh und ungefüge. Ihre Kleidung sind Schurze von Wildkatzenpelzen, Joppen von Baumrinden und Zottelschurze von Füchsen und anderm Getier.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mata mereka gelap sehitam arang tetapi kadang menyala dan memancarkan kilat; suara mereka suara lelaki yang kasar; pakaiannya celemek kulit kucing hutan, jaket kulit kayu, dan celemek bulu rubah.",
        "en": "Their eyes are dark and coal-black but sometimes glow and flash; their voice is a man's, rough and coarse; they wear aprons of wildcat fur, jackets of tree bark and shaggy fox-skin aprons."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c13",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s2",
      "quote": "Sie werden oft mit den Zwergen, Gnomen und derlei gleichgesetzt, sollen aber, trotz ihrer geringen Körpergröße, über gewaltige Kräfte verfügen. Außerdem heißt es, dass sie Gämse und Wölfe zähmen, deren Milch trinken und sie gar als Reittiere nutzen sollen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fängge kecil sering disamakan dengan kurcaci dan gnome tetapi, meski bertubuh kecil, sangat kuat; mereka menjinakkan chamois dan serigala, meminum susunya, bahkan menungganginya.",
        "en": "The small Fängge are often equated with dwarfs and gnomes but, despite their size, are immensely strong; they tame chamois and wolves, drink their milk and even ride them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fanggen-c14",
      "source_id": "fanggen-s2",
      "quote": "„Im Walde, der von Nassereit nach Stra sich zieht, hauste eine Fangg, welche von der Größe eines mittelmäßigen Baumes war. Sie lauerte stets auf Menschen, und wenn sie kleine Buben zu fassen bekam, so schnupfte sie dieselben in ihre Nase",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda Tirol (Tiroler): di hutan antara Nassereit dan Stra tinggal seekor Fangg setinggi pohon sedang yang selalu mengintai manusia, dan bila menangkap anak lelaki kecil, ia mengisapnya ke dalam hidungnya seperti tembakau.",
        "en": "A Tyrolean (Tiroler) legend: in the forest from Nassereit to Stra lived a Fangg as tall as a middling tree, always lurking for people; when she caught little boys she snuffed them up her nose."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",

    },
    {
      "field": "images",

    }
  ]
}

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Fenixmännlein adalah roh tanah dan kurcaci dalam cerita rakyat Jerman dari Silesia dan Lusatia, penghuni bukit yang terkenal suka memanggang kue dan menukar bayi manusia dengan anak tukar.",
    "en": "The Fenixmännlein are earth sprites and dwarfs of German folklore from Silesia and Lusatia, hill-dwellers famed for baking cake and for swapping human babies with changelings.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "fenixmannlein-c01",
      "fenixmannlein-c03",
      "fenixmannlein-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Fenixmännlein, juga disebut Fenixmännel, Fenskemännel, atau Fenesleute, adalah roh tanah dan kurcaci dari cerita rakyat Jerman yang dahulu dikenal di Silesia, terutama di Oderské vrchy dan Hrubý Jeseník, serta di bagian Lusatia milik Sachsen. Mereka lebih besar dari kurcaci tetapi lebih kecil dari manusia, sehingga namanya mungkin terkait dengan Venedigermännlein, \"lelaki kecil dari Venesia\". Mereka hidup berkelompok di bukit dan semak.",
      "en": "The Fenixmännlein, also called Fenixmännel, Fenskemännel or Fenesleute, are earth sprites and dwarfs of German folklore once known in Silesia, especially the Oderské vrchy and Hrubý Jeseník, and in the Saxon part of Lusatia. They are bigger than dwarfs but smaller than people, so their name may be linked with the Venedigermännlein, 'little Venetian men'. They live in groups in hills and shrubbery.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "fenixmannlein-c01",
        "fenixmannlein-c02",
        "fenixmannlein-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Di dalam gunung mereka berpesta besar, dan mereka konon pemilik asli resep kue streusel Silesia; asap yang naik dari lubang tanah menandakan mereka sedang memanggang. Para petani yang meminta sepotong kue kadang mendapatkannya, tetapi harus memakannya di atas \"meja besi\", misalnya mata bajak, dan kue itu bisa berubah menjadi timah, debu, atau emas.",
      "en": "Deep inside the mountains they hold great feasts, and Silesian streuselkuchen is said to have been their recipe first; whenever smoke drifts up from holes in the ground, they are baking. Farmhands who asked for a piece of cake were sometimes given one, but had to eat it on an 'iron table', such as a ploughshare, and the cake might turn to lead, dust or gold.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "fenixmannlein-c03",
        "fenixmannlein-c05",
        "fenixmannlein-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mereka kadang menukar bayi manusia dengan anak tukar mereka sendiri dan suka menculik perempuan yang baru melahirkan untuk mengasuh anak-anak mereka. Untuk menyelamatkan korban, sang penyelamat harus berkuda secepatnya sambil melempar rami, sisir, dan sabun di belakangnya agar para kurcaci sibuk. Menurut berbagai tradisi, mereka akhirnya meninggalkan negeri itu karena jalur kereta api, peterseli dalam masakan, atau dentang lonceng gereja.",
      "en": "They sometimes swap human babies for their own changelings and like to abduct women in childbed to nurse their children. To save a victim, the rescuer must ride as fast as he can, throwing flax, a comb and soap behind him to keep the dwarfs busy. Various traditions say they finally left the land because of the railways, parsley in people's food or the ringing of church bells.",
      "claim_ids": [

        "fenixmannlein-c08",
        "fenixmannlein-c09",
        "fenixmannlein-c10",
        "fenixmannlein-c11"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c01",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "are earth sprites and dwarves from German folklore. They were historically known in Silesia, particularly in the Oderské vrchy and Hrubý Jeseník as well as in the part of Lusatia belonging to Saxony.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fenixmännlein (juga Fenixmännel, Fenskemännel, Fenesleute) adalah roh tanah dan kurcaci dari cerita rakyat Jerman (German), dahulu dikenal di Silesia, terutama Oderské vrchy dan Hrubý Jeseník, serta bagian Lusatia milik Sachsen (Saxony).",
        "en": "The Fenixmännlein (also Fenixmännel, Fenskemännel, Fenesleute) are earth sprites and dwarfs of German folklore, once known in Silesia, especially the Oderské vrchy and Hrubý Jeseník, and in the Saxon part of Lusatia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c02",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "The latter option is supported by the fact that both Fenixmännlein and Venedigermännlein are supposed to be bigger than dwarves (German Zwerge, sg. Zwerg) but smaller than humans. The Fenixmännlein are collective beings dwelling in hills and shrubbery.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Asal nama dari Venedigermännlein (\"lelaki kecil dari Venesia\") didukung fakta bahwa keduanya lebih besar dari kurcaci tetapi lebih kecil dari manusia; Fenixmännlein hidup berkelompok di bukit dan semak.",
        "en": "Derivation from the Venedigermännlein ('little Venetian men') is supported since both are bigger than dwarfs but smaller than humans; the Fenixmännlein live collectively in hills and shrubbery."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c03",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "Inside the mountains, they throw big parties. The Fenismannla (pl. Mannla = little men in Silesian German) are said to be the original owners of the recipe for Silesian streuselkuchen. When the Fänismännchen (sg., pl.;Männchen = little man) are baking cake in their caves, smoke is rising from earth holes and from beneath clods.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di dalam gunung mereka berpesta besar; mereka konon pemilik asli resep kue streusel Silesia (Silesian streuselkuchen), dan saat mereka memanggang di gua, asap naik dari lubang tanah.",
        "en": "Inside the mountains they hold big parties; they are said to own the original Silesian streuselkuchen recipe, and when they bake in their caves smoke rises from earth holes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c04",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "The Fenixmännlein are known to occasionally exchange human children for their own which are left behind as changelings.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fenixmännlein kadang menukar anak manusia dengan anak mereka sendiri, yang ditinggalkan sebagai anak tukar.",
        "en": "The Fenixmännlein occasionally swap human children for their own, left behind as changelings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c05",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "Immediately, an invisible hand gave him a Krautkuchen (cabbage cake) coated with green linseed oil. At the same time, he got threatened to eat this cake on a table made from iron or else he would be done for. The ploughboy fulfilled the condition by using his ploughshare as the iron table.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seorang pembajak yang meminta kue segera diberi kue kubis berlapis minyak biji rami oleh tangan tak terlihat, dengan ancaman harus memakannya di atas meja besi; ia memakai mata bajaknya sebagai meja besi.",
        "en": "A ploughboy who asked for cake was at once handed a cabbage cake with linseed oil by an invisible hand, threatened to eat it on an iron table; he used his ploughshare as that table."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c06",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "The cake of the Fenixmannla turns to lead inside the stomach. The Krautplatz (cabbage cake) of the Fenesleute dwelling in the Fenesloch (Fenes Hole) turns to dust. The cake of the Fenixmännchen might also turn to gold.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kue Fenixmännlein bisa berubah menjadi timah di dalam perut, menjadi debu (kue Fenesleute dari Fenesloch), atau menjadi emas.",
        "en": "The Fenixmännlein's cake may turn to lead in the stomach, to dust (that of the Fenesleute of the Fenesloch), or to gold."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c07",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s2",
      "quote": "Als Fenixmännlein (Fenixmännel, Fenskemännel, Fenisleute) bezeichnet man eine Gruppe schlesischer zwergenhafter Dämonen, die mit den Erdgeistern und Zwergen verwandt sein sollen, da sie denselben Beschäftigungen nachgehen. Wie die meisten Naturgeister leben sie in menschlicher Nähe und versuchen, die Kinder der Menschen mit ihren Wechselbälgern zu vertauschen. Selten sind sie bösartig.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fenixmännlein (Fenixmännel, Fenskemännel, Fenisleute) adalah kelompok setan kerdil Silesia (schlesischer) yang dianggap berkerabat dengan roh tanah dan kurcaci; seperti kebanyakan roh alam mereka tinggal dekat manusia dan mencoba menukar anak manusia dengan anak tukar, tetapi jarang jahat.",
        "en": "Fenixmännlein (Fenixmännel, Fenskemännel, Fenisleute) are a group of Silesian (schlesischer) dwarfish demons thought related to earth spirits and dwarfs; like most nature spirits they live near people and try to swap human children with changelings, but are rarely malicious."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c08",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "The rescuer will need to ride his horse as fast as he can, all the while throwing behind him flax (the dwarves must pull) a comb (the dwarves must heckle the flax with) and soap (the dwarves will slip on).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyelamat perempuan yang diculik harus berkuda secepatnya sambil melempar rami, sisir, dan sabun ke belakang.",
        "en": "The rescuer of an abducted woman must ride as fast as possible, throwing flax, a comb and soap behind him."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c09",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "In Austrian Silesia, the Fenesleute have moved out of the country. This happened because people started adding parsley to their dishes which they were unable to tolerate. The introduction railways also made the Fenesleute migrate",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Silesia Austria, Fenesleute meninggalkan negeri karena orang mulai menambahkan peterseli ke masakan, yang tak mereka tahan, dan karena hadirnya jalur kereta api.",
        "en": "In Austrian Silesia the Fenesleute left because people began adding parsley, which they could not stand, to their food, and because of the railways."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c10",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "In Lusatia, the Venusmännel or Feensmännel migrated westwards after Ostritz had got its bell tower. The dwarves could not stand the sound of the large bell in particular.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Lusatia, Venusmännel atau Feensmännel pindah ke barat setelah Ostritz memiliki menara lonceng, karena para kurcaci tak tahan suara lonceng besarnya.",
        "en": "In Lusatia the Venusmännel or Feensmännel moved west after Ostritz got its bell tower, as the dwarfs could not stand the big bell."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c11",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "The Fenixmännlein particularly like abducting women in childbed, carrying them off to the Fenixmannlaberg (Fenix Men Mountain) to care for their children or for washing.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fenixmännlein terutama suka menculik perempuan yang baru melahirkan dan membawanya ke Fenixmannlaberg untuk mengasuh anak-anak mereka atau mencuci.",
        "en": "The Fenixmännlein especially like abducting women in childbed to the Fenixmannlaberg to nurse their children or wash."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fenixmannlein-c12",
      "source_id": "fenixmannlein-s1",
      "quote": "But not only the male Fenixmännlein know how to bake, their female equivalent, the Venusweiblein (sg., pl.; Weiblein = little woman), do too.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bukan hanya Fenixmännlein lelaki yang pandai memanggang; padanan perempuan mereka, Venusweiblein, juga.",
        "en": "Not only the male Fenixmännlein can bake; their female counterparts, the Venusweiblein, can too."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Feuermann (\"manusia api\") adalah hantu berapi dalam cerita rakyat Jerman, arwah pendosa yang menebus kesalahannya dan muncul di ladang pada malam hari, lebih besar daripada api pelita (will-o'-the-wisp).",
    "en": "The Feuermann ('fire man') is a fiery ghost of German folklore, the soul of a sinner atoning for its misdeeds who appears in the fields at night, larger than a will-o'-the-wisp.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "feuermann-ghost-c01",
      "feuermann-ghost-c04",
      "feuermann-ghost-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Feuermann, juga disebut Brennender, Brünnling, Züsler, Glühender, atau Feuerputz, adalah hantu berapi dalam cerita rakyat Jerman yang dibedakan dari Irrlicht (api pelita) karena ukurannya yang besar. Wujudnya beragam: kerangka manusia yang menyala dari dalam, lelaki hitam dalam tiang api, penunggang tanpa kepala di atas kuda berapi, atau pembajak berapi; ia juga bisa berupa berkas jerami raksasa, bola api, atau roda api, dan dapat mengubah ukurannya sesuka hati.",
      "en": "The Feuermann, also called Brennender, Brünnling, Züsler, Glühender or Feuerputz, is a fiery ghost of German folklore set apart from the Irrlicht (will-o'-the-wisp) by its large size. Its forms vary: a human skeleton ablaze inside, a black man within a pillar of fire, a headless rider on a fiery horse or a fiery ploughman; it may also be a giant sheaf of straw, a fireball or a fiery wheel, and can change its size at will.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "feuermann-ghost-c01",
        "feuermann-ghost-c02",
        "feuermann-ghost-c03",
        "feuermann-ghost-c06",
        "feuermann-ghost-c12"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia dianggap pendosa yang masih bergentayangan di bumi untuk menebus dosa-dosanya; nyalanya adalah api penyuciannya. Sering ia seorang Grenzfrevler, orang yang dulu memindahkan batu batas dengan niat jahat dan kini harus menghantui batas itu, tetapi bisa juga koster pencuri uang kolekte, pembakar, atau pengkhianat. Ia hanya muncul pada sore atau malam hari, terutama di ladang dan batas ladang serta padang rumput berawa.",
      "en": "It is seen as a sinner still haunting the earth to atone for the sins of its life; its fire is its purgatory. Often it is a Grenzfrevler, someone who moved a boundary stone with criminal intent and must now haunt that boundary, but it may also be a sexton who stole from the collection bag, an arsonist or a traitor. It appears only in the evening or at night, mostly in fields, field borders and swampy meadows.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "feuermann-ghost-c04",
        "feuermann-ghost-c05",
        "feuermann-ghost-c07",
        "feuermann-ghost-c15"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Biasanya tak berbahaya, Feuermann bahkan menerangi jalan orang pada malam hari dengan imbalan sedikit uang atau ucapan \"Vergelt's Gott\", yang sudah cukup untuk menebusnya. Namun ia membalas bila imbalan yang dijanjikan tidak diberikan atau bila dipermainkan: menyesatkan orang, melompat ke punggung mereka, atau membakar jerami di gerobak bahkan rumah mereka. Menyentuh tangannya berbahaya, karena akan meninggalkan cap tangan hitam yang terbakar.",
      "en": "Usually harmless, the Feuermann may even light people's way at night for a small coin or the words 'Vergelt's Gott', which is enough to redeem it. But it takes revenge if denied a promised reward or made a fool of: it leads people astray, leaps onto their backs, or sets the straw on their wagon or even their house alight. Touching its hand is dangerous, as it leaves a black, burnt handprint.",

        "feuermann-ghost-c09",
        "feuermann-ghost-c10",
        "feuermann-ghost-c11",
        "feuermann-ghost-c14"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c01",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "is a fiery ghost from German folklore, which is different from the will-o'-the-wisp (German Irrlicht) owing to its size: Feuermänner are rather big, while Irrlichter are rather small.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Feuermann (\"manusia api\"; juga Brennender, Brünnling, Züsler, Glühender, glühende Männer) adalah hantu berapi dari cerita rakyat Jerman (German) yang berbeda dari will-o'-the-wisp (Irrlicht) karena ukurannya: Feuermänner agak besar, Irrlichter agak kecil.",
        "en": "The Feuermann ('fire man'; also Brennender, Brünnling, Züsler, Glühender, glühende Männer) is a fiery ghost of German folklore, unlike the will-o'-the-wisp (Irrlicht) in size: Feuermänner are big, Irrlichter small."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c02",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "Some are described as rather humanoid, such as a human skeleton whose insides are blazing with fire, or as a leaden manikin wearing a leaden mantle blazing with fire underneath. Another description is of a black man inside a tall, wide pillar of fire, or a Feuermann who is half black, half fiery. It is able to change its height at will, varying from gigantic to diminutive.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian digambarkan mirip manusia, seperti kerangka yang menyala dari dalam atau lelaki kecil bermantel timah dengan api di bawahnya; ada pula lelaki hitam di dalam tiang api; ia dapat mengubah tingginya sesuka hati dari raksasa hingga kecil.",
        "en": "Some are humanlike, such as a skeleton blazing inside or a leaden manikin with fire under its mantle; others a black man in a pillar of fire; it can change its height at will from gigantic to tiny."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c03",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "Alternatively, the Feuermann also appears more or less shapeless like a (sometimes gigantic) sheaf or bundle of straw, a big floating light, a fiery ball, a pyramid, a snake, or a fiery wheel. The Feuermann is very quick and able to rise in the air so that it hovers above the treetops.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Feuermann juga bisa tampil hampir tanpa bentuk, seperti berkas jerami (kadang raksasa), cahaya besar melayang, bola api, piramida, ular, atau roda api; ia sangat cepat dan dapat naik ke udara melayang di atas pucuk pohon.",
        "en": "The Feuermann may also appear nearly shapeless, as a (sometimes giant) sheaf of straw, a big floating light, a fireball, a pyramid, a snake or a fiery wheel; it is very fast and can rise to hover above the treetops."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c04",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "It is seen as a sinner remaining hauntingly on earth to repent for its sins during lifetime. Frequently, the Feuermann is a so-called Grenzfrevler (sg., pl.) or boundary sinner who has moved a boundary stone or similar landmark with criminal intent, thus damning the soul to haunt the very boundary it unlawfully changed in life.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Feuermann dianggap pendosa yang bergentayangan di bumi untuk menebus dosa semasa hidup; sering ia seorang Grenzfrevler yang memindahkan batu batas dengan niat jahat sehingga jiwanya dikutuk menghantui batas itu.",
        "en": "The Feuermann is seen as a sinner haunting the earth to atone for its life's sins; often a Grenzfrevler who moved a boundary stone with criminal intent and is damned to haunt that boundary."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c05",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "The Feuermann appears only at evening or at night, preferably at fields, particularly field borders, and meadows, especially swampy ones.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Feuermann hanya muncul pada sore atau malam hari, terutama di ladang, khususnya batas ladang, dan padang rumput, terutama yang berawa.",
        "en": "The Feuermann appears only in the evening or at night, preferably in fields, especially field borders, and meadows, particularly swampy ones."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c06",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s2",
      "quote": "Der Feuerputz, der auch Brünnling, Feuerpütz, Feurputz, Glühender, Züsler, am häufigsten aber Feuermann genannt wird, ist ein feuriger Spuk und eine Sagengestalt.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Feuerputz, juga disebut Brünnling, Feuerpütz, Glühender, Züsler, tetapi paling sering Feuermann, adalah hantu berapi dan tokoh legenda.",
        "en": "The Feuerputz, also called Brünnling, Feuerpütz, Glühender, Züsler, but most often Feuermann, is a fiery spook and legendary figure."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c07",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s2",
      "quote": "Feuermänner sind Seelen, die sich im Leben versündigt haben und dies nun abbüßen müssen. Ihre feurige Erscheinung ist also ihr Fegefeuer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Feuermänner adalah jiwa yang berdosa semasa hidup dan kini harus menebusnya; wujud berapi mereka adalah api penyucian mereka.",
        "en": "Feuermänner are souls who sinned in life and must now atone; their fiery form is their purgatory."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c08",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "Usually harmless, the Feuermann can also appear helpful to humans by showing and illuminating them the way at night for a small sum of money or a word of gratitude such as \"Vergelt's Gott\" (\"May God reward it\") or \"Bezahl dir's Gott (soviel Mal wie du's nötig hast)\" (\"May God repay you (as many times as you need)\"). Such a blessing redeems the burning sinner",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Biasanya tak berbahaya, Feuermann bisa menolong dengan menerangi jalan pada malam hari demi sedikit uang atau ucapan terima kasih seperti \"Vergelt's Gott\" (\"semoga Tuhan membalasnya\"); berkat semacam itu menebus pendosa yang terbakar itu.",
        "en": "Usually harmless, the Feuermann may help by lighting the way at night for a small coin or thanks such as 'Vergelt's Gott'; such a blessing redeems the burning sinner."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c09",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "A malicious Feuermann can be experienced when it has helped out a human and is refused the promised reward or when it takes revenge on a human who has played a prank on it. Then, the Feuermann leads astray, practices \"aufhocken\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Feuermann menjadi jahat bila imbalan yang dijanjikan tidak diberikan atau bila membalas orang yang mempermainkannya; ia lalu menyesatkan orang dan melompat ke punggung mereka (aufhocken).",
        "en": "A Feuermann turns malicious when refused a promised reward or taking revenge on a prankster; then it leads people astray and leaps onto their backs (aufhocken)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c10",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "It is dangerous to touch the Feuermann's hand. Thus, one should always give an object instead of one's hand as one will find a black handprint burnt on it where the Feuermann has touched.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menyentuh tangan Feuermann berbahaya; karena itu orang sebaiknya menyodorkan benda alih-alih tangannya, sebab akan tertinggal cap tangan hitam yang terbakar di tempat yang disentuhnya.",
        "en": "Touching the Feuermann's hand is dangerous; so one should offer an object instead of one's hand, as a black handprint will be burnt where it touched."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c11",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s2",
      "quote": "Schon ein schlichtes „Vergelt's Gott“ soll dabei ausreichen, um den Feuermann zu erlösen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ucapan sederhana \"Vergelt's Gott\" konon sudah cukup untuk menebus Feuermann.",
        "en": "A simple 'Vergelt's Gott' is said to be enough to redeem the Feuermann."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c12",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "Rarer appearances are that of a fiery, sometimes headless rider on a fiery horse and that of a fiery ploughman with fiery horse and plough.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penampakan yang lebih jarang adalah penunggang berapi, kadang tanpa kepala, di atas kuda berapi, dan pembajak berapi dengan kuda dan bajak berapi.",
        "en": "Rarer forms are a fiery, sometimes headless rider on a fiery horse and a fiery ploughman with fiery horse and plough."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c13",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "Such a blessing redeems the burning sinner, as do prayers and masses because of which Feuermänner draw near upon hearing a prayer. Swearing drives them away instead.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Berkat semacam itu menebus pendosa yang terbakar, begitu pula doa dan misa, sehingga Feuermänner mendekat bila mendengar doa; sebaliknya umpatan mengusir mereka.",
        "en": "Such a blessing redeems the burning sinner, as do prayers and masses, so Feuermänner approach on hearing a prayer; swearing drives them away."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c14",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "or follows the person and sets the straw on their wagon or even their house ablaze.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Atau ia mengikuti orang itu dan membakar jerami di gerobak bahkan rumahnya.",
        "en": "Or it follows the person and sets the straw on their wagon or even their house on fire."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "feuermann-ghost-c15",
      "source_id": "feuermann-ghost-s1",
      "quote": "Other possible identities of the Feuermann include a sacristan who has stolen money from the collection bag, a murderous incendiary, a traitor of his country, and several other kinds of evildoers.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Identitas lain Feuermann termasuk koster yang mencuri uang kantong kolekte, pembakar pembunuh, pengkhianat negeri, dan berbagai penjahat lain.",
        "en": "Other possible identities of the Feuermann include a sacristan who stole from the collection bag, a murderous arsonist, a traitor to his country and other evildoers."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",

    },
    {
      "field": "images",

    }
  ]
}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Finngálkn adalah makhluk dongeng Nordik dan Islandia, campuran manusia di bagian atas dan hewan di bagian bawah seperti kentaur, yang dalam tradisi kemudian berarti hewan buas hasil kawin kucing dan rubah.",
    "en": "The Finngálkn is a fabulous beast of Norse and Icelandic lore, human above and animal below like a centaur, which in later tradition meant a fierce offspring of a cat and a fox.",

    {
      "id": "Finngálkn adalah makhluk ajaib yang disebut dalam beberapa saga legendaris (fornaldarsögur): sejenis kimera, biasanya campuran manusia dan hewan, manusia di bagian atas dan hewan di bagian bawah, seperti kentaur. Terjemahan Islandia abad ke-12 atas bestiari Physiologus sudah menyamakan finngálkn dengan onocentaurus.",
      "en": "The Finngálkn is a wondrous beast mentioned in several legendary sagas (fornaldarsögur): a kind of chimera, usually a blend of human and animal, human above and beast below, like a centaur. The 12th-century Icelandic translation of the bestiary Physiologus already equates finngálkn with the onocentaur.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "finngalkn-c01",
        "finngalkn-c03",
        "finngalkn-c04"
      ]

    {
      "id": "Dalam Hjálmþés saga ok Ölvis, sang pangeran Hjálmþér terpaksa mencium finngálkn bernama Vargeisa, yang ternyata putri yang terkena sihir; ia berekor dan berkuku kuda, bersurai panjang, bermata terang, bermulut lebar, dan bertangan besar. Dalam Örvar-Odds saga, Grímhildr, ibu Ögmundr, berubah menjadi finngálkn berkepala manusia, bertubuh hewan, bercakar sangat besar, dan berekor raksasa. Njáls saga menceritakan Þorkell hákr yang bertarung lama dan akhirnya membunuh seekor finngálkn.",
      "en": "In Hjálmþés saga ok Ölvis, Prince Hjálmþér is forced to kiss the finngálkn Vargeisa, who turns out to be a bewitched princess; she has a horse's tail and hooves, a long mane, bright eyes, a wide mouth and big hands. In Örvar-Odds saga, Grímhildr, Ögmundr's mother, turns into a finngálkn with a human head, an animal body, huge claws and an enormous tail. Njáls saga tells of Þorkell hákr fighting a finngálkn long and finally killing it.",

      "id": "Dalam kumpulan cerita rakyat Jón Árnason, finngálkn adalah hewan hasil kawin kucing dan rubah, sangat buas dan lebih berbahaya bagi domba daripada pemangsa lain, pemalu, sangat cepat, dan hanya bisa ditembak dengan kancing atau peluru perak.       "en": "In Jón Árnason's folktale collection, the finngálkn is the offspring of a cat and a fox, very fierce and more harmful to sheep than any other predator, shy, extremely fast, and able to be shot only with a silver button or silver bullet.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c01",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s1",
      "quote": "Finngálkn er furðusagnakvikindi, maður að ofan en dýr að neðan (einnig kallað Kentári). Finngálkn er einnig haft um afkvæmi tófu og kattar.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Finngálkn adalah makhluk dongeng, manusia di atas dan hewan di bawah (juga disebut Kentári, kentaur); kata finngálkn juga dipakai untuk keturunan rubah dan kucing.",
        "en": "The Finngálkn is a fabulous beast, human above and animal below (also called Kentári, centaur); the word also denotes the offspring of a fox and a cat."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c02",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s1",
      "quote": "Finngálkn er það dýr kallað, sem köttur og tófa geta saman. Er það grimmt mjög, og öllum vargi skaðlegra fyrir sauðfé manna, og skotharðast allra dýra. Vinnur engin kúla á Finngálknið, og verður það ekki skotið nema með silfurhnapp eða silfurkúlu.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kumpulan cerita Jón Árnason (Þjóðsögum Jóns Árnasonar), finngálkn adalah hewan hasil kawin kucing dan rubah, sangat buas, lebih berbahaya bagi domba daripada pemangsa lain, dan paling sulit ditembak; tak ada peluru yang mempan kecuali kancing atau peluru perak.",
        "en": "Per Jón Árnason's folktales (Þjóðsögum Jóns Árnasonar), the finngálkn is the beast a cat and fox beget, very fierce, more harmful to sheep than any other predator and hardest of all to shoot; no bullet works on it but a silver button or bullet."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c03",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s2",
      "quote": "Finngálkn är ett fabeldjur som omtalas i flera fornaldarsagor. Det rör sig om en chimär; oftast en blandning mellan människa och djur.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Finngálkn adalah hewan dongeng yang disebut dalam beberapa saga legendaris (fornaldarsagor), sejenis kimera, biasanya campuran manusia dan hewan.",
        "en": "The Finngálkn is a fabulous beast named in several legendary sagas (fornaldarsagor), a chimera, usually a mix of human and animal."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c04",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s2",
      "quote": "I den isländska översättningen av bestiariet Physiologus, gjord på 1100-talet men endast bevarad i två 1200-talsfragment, sägs finngálkn vara en kentaur",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam terjemahan Islandia (isländska) bestiari Physiologus dari abad ke-12, yang hanya tersisa dalam dua fragmen abad ke-13, finngálkn disebut kentaur (onocentaurus).",
        "en": "In the 12th-century Icelandic (isländska) translation of the bestiary Physiologus, surviving only in two 13th-century fragments, finngálkn is said to be a centaur (onocentaurus)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c05",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s2",
      "quote": "Om Ögmundr Eyþjófsbanis mor Grímhildr sägs i Örvar-Odds saga att hon vid en viss tidpunkt hade förvandlats till finngálkn. Hennes huvud var som en människas, men bakkroppen ett djurs. Hon hade mycket stora klor och en väldig svans.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Örvar-Odds saga menyebut Grímhildr, ibu Ögmundr Eyþjófsbani, pernah berubah menjadi finngálkn: kepalanya seperti manusia, tubuh belakangnya hewan, bercakar sangat besar dan berekor raksasa.",
        "en": "Örvar-Odds saga says Grímhildr, mother of Ögmundr Eyþjófsbani, once turned into a finngálkn: her head human, her hind body an animal's, with very large claws and a huge tail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c06",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s2",
      "quote": "En hona, lystrande till namnet Vargeisa, som tycks ha tillhört ”hästkentaurerna”, omtalas i Hjálmþés saga ok Ölvis. ”Hon hade hästsvans, hovar och lång man; ögonen var ljusa, munnen bred och händerna stora.”",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Hjálmþés saga ok Ölvis disebut finngálkn betina bernama Vargeisa, tampaknya \"kentaur kuda\": berekor dan berkuku kuda, bersurai panjang, bermata terang, bermulut lebar, bertangan besar.",
        "en": "Hjálmþés saga ok Ölvis mentions a female named Vargeisa, seemingly a 'horse-centaur': horse's tail and hooves, long mane, bright eyes, wide mouth and large hands."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c07",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s2",
      "quote": "Etymologin för ordet finngálkn är okänd. Ordet associerar till ”onaturlig blandning”.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Etimologi kata finngálkn tidak diketahui; kata itu dikaitkan dengan \"campuran yang tak wajar\".",
        "en": "The etymology of finngálkn is unknown; the word is associated with 'unnatural mixture'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c08",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s2",
      "quote": "Under medeltiden var Finngálkn ett norrönt namn på stjärnbilden Sagittarius.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada Abad Pertengahan, Finngálkn adalah nama Nordik (norrönt) untuk rasi Sagitarius (Sagittarius).",
        "en": "In the Middle Ages Finngálkn was a Norse (norrönt) name for the constellation Sagittarius."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c09",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s2",
      "quote": "Þá mœtti hann finngálkni og varðisk því lengi, en svá lauk með þeim, at hann drap finngálknit.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Njáls saga, Þorkell hákr bertemu finngálkn, lama bertarung dengannya, dan akhirnya membunuhnya.",
        "en": "In Njáls saga Þorkell hákr met a finngálkn, fought it long and finally killed it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c10",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s1",
      "quote": "Í Hjálmþés sögu og Ölvis neyðist konungssonurinn Hjálmþés til að kyssa finngálknið Vargeisu. Hún reynist þó vera prinsessa í álögum.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Hjálmþés saga ok Ölvis, pangeran Hjálmþér terpaksa mencium finngálkn Vargeisa, yang ternyata putri yang terkena sihir.",
        "en": "In Hjálmþés saga ok Ölvis Prince Hjálmþér is forced to kiss the finngálkn Vargeisa, who proves to be a bewitched princess."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "finngalkn-c11",
      "source_id": "finngalkn-s2",
      "quote": "Sagittarius avbildades ofta som en bågskjutande kentaur, varför denna stjärnbild – förutom de mer korrekta benämningarna Bogmaðr eller Skyti – också kom att kallas Finngálkn.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sagitarius sering digambarkan sebagai kentaur pemanah, sehingga rasi ini, selain disebut Bogmaðr atau Skyti, juga dinamai Finngálkn.",
        "en": "Sagittarius was often shown as an archer centaur, so the constellation, besides Bogmaðr or Skyti, came to be called Finngálkn."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Fjalar adalah ayam jantan merah dalam mitologi Nordik yang berkokok menandai datangnya Ragnarök, menurut Völuspá.",
    "en": "Fjalar is the red rooster of Norse mythology whose crowing heralds the coming of Ragnarök, according to Völuspá.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Fjalar (bahasa Nordik Kuno Fjalarr) adalah ayam jantan merah mitis yang dikatakan menandai awal Ragnarök dalam mitologi Nordik. Dalam Völuspá ia digambarkan sebagai ayam jantan merah yang indah, berkokok di \"hutan tiang gantungan\" (gaglviðr) di dekat Eggþér, gembala raksasa perempuan, yang dengan riang memetik harpanya di atas sebuah gundukan.",
      "en": "Fjalar (Old Norse Fjalarr) is the mythical red rooster said to herald the onset of Ragnarök in Norse mythology.       "id": "Nama Fjalarr diterjemahkan \"penipu\" atau \"penyembunyi\" dan mungkin berasal dari bentuk Proto-Nordik *felaraʀ. Peter H. Salus dan Paul B. Taylor berpendapat hanya sang kurcaci yang benar-benar bernama Fjalar, sedangkan ayam jantan dan kedua raksasa disebut demikian karena kelicikan mereka.",
      "en": "The name Fjalarr is translated 'deceiver' or 'hider' and probably comes from a Proto-Norse form *felaraʀ.       "source_id": "fjalar-s1",
      "quote": "Fjalar (Old Norse: Fjalarr [ˈfjɑlɑrː] , \"deceiver\") is the mythical red rooster that is said to herald the onset of Ragnarök in Norse mythology.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fjalar (Nordik Kuno: Fjalarr, \"penipu\") adalah ayam jantan merah mitis yang dikatakan menandai awal Ragnarök dalam mitologi Nordik (Norse).",
        "en": "Fjalar (Old Norse Fjalarr, 'deceiver') is the mythical red rooster said to herald the onset of Ragnarök in Norse mythology."

      "id": "fjalar-c02",
      "source_id": "fjalar-s1",
      "quote": "The Old Norse name Fjalarr has been translated as 'deceiver' or 'hider'. It probably derives from an earlier Proto-Norse form reconstructed as *felaraʀ.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Nordik Kuno Fjalarr diterjemahkan \"penipu\" atau \"penyembunyi\" dan mungkin berasal dari bentuk Proto-Nordik (Proto-Norse) *felaraʀ.",

      "source_id": "fjalar-s1",
      "quote": "Three other unrelated figures bear the name Fjalar in Norse mythology.       "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tiga tokoh lain yang tak berkaitan juga bernama Fjalar: nama lain Suttungr dalam versi Hávamál tentang pencurian mead puisi oleh Odin, dan salah satu kurcaci pembuat mead puisi dari darah Kvasir dalam Skáldskaparmál.",
        "en": "Three other unrelated figures are called Fjalar: an alias of Suttungr in Hávamál's version of Odin's theft of the mead of poetry, one of the dwarfs who made the mead from Kvasir's blood in Skáldskaparmál."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fjalar-c04",
      "source_id": "fjalar-s1",
      "quote": "Peter H. Salus and Paul B. Taylor argue that \"of these four, probably only one (the dwarf) is actually named Fjalar, the cock and the two giants are called Fjalar because of their cunning.\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {

      "id": "fjalar-c05",
      "source_id": "fjalar-s1",
      "quote": "In Völuspá (Prophecy of the Völva), Fjalar is portrayed as a beautiful red rooster who crows the onset of Ragnarök, sitting near the herdsman Eggþér who is joyfully striking his harp.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Völuspá, Fjalar digambarkan sebagai ayam jantan merah yang indah yang berkokok menandai awal Ragnarök, di dekat gembala Eggþér yang dengan riang memetik harpanya.",
        "en": "In Völuspá Fjalar is shown as a beautiful red rooster crowing in the onset of Ragnarök near the herdsman Eggþér, who joyfully strikes his harp."

      "source_id": "fjalar-s2",
      "quote": "I nordisk mytologi (Vølvens spådom) er Fjalar en rød hane, der galer i et træ og dermed indvarsler Ragnarok.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Nordik (nordisk; Vølvens spådom atau Völuspá), Fjalar adalah ayam jantan merah yang berkokok di sebatang pohon dan dengan demikian menandai Ragnarok.",
        "en": "In Norse (nordisk) mythology (Vølvens spådom, Völuspá) Fjalar is a red rooster who crows in a tree and so heralds Ragnarok."

      "id": "fjalar-c07",
      "source_id": "fjalar-s1",
      "quote": "Finally, in Hárbarðsljóð ('The Lay of Hárbarðr'), the jötunn who succeeded in deceiving Thor is also named Fjalar.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Hárbarðsljóð, jötunn yang berhasil menipu Thor juga bernama Fjalar.",
        "en": "In Hárbarðsljóð the jötunn who succeeded in deceiving Thor is also named Fjalar."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "fjalar-c08",
      "source_id": "fjalar-s1",
      "quote": "Sat þar á haugi ok sló hörpu gýgjar hirðir glaðr Egðir; gól um hánum í gaglviði fagrrauðr hani, sá er Fjalarr heitir .",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Völuspá 42: di atas gundukan duduk Egðir, gembala raksasa perempuan, memetik harpa dengan riang; di atasnya, di gaglviðr (\"hutan tiang gantungan\"), berkokok ayam jantan merah indah bernama Fjalarr.",
        "en": "Völuspá 42: on the mound sat glad Egðir, herdsman of the giantess, striking his harp; above him in the gaglviðr ('gallows-wood') crowed the fair red cock called Fjalarr."

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Flodder atau Flodderduivel adalah roh pengganggu dalam cerita rakyat Brabant Barat, Belanda, yang melompat dari air ke punggung pejalan malam dan membebani mereka seberat timah.",
    "en": "The Flodder or Flodderduivel is a tormenting spirit of West Brabant folklore in the Netherlands that leaps from the water onto night walkers' backs and weighs on them like lead.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "flodder-c01",
      "flodder-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Flodder, juga disebut Flodderduivel, adalah roh pengganggu dari cerita rakyat Brabant Barat. Ia tinggal di parit, kolam, dan di sepanjang tanggul, mengintai dari air pejalan malam, lalu melompat ke punggung mereka dan minta digendong sebagai beban yang sangat berat. Begitu korbannya mencapai permukiman, roh itu tiba-tiba lenyap. Namanya berasal dari bunyi yang ia timbulkan saat muncul dari air.",
      "en": "The Flodder, also called Flodderduivel, is a tormenting spirit of West Brabant folklore. It lives in ditches, pools and along dykes, watches night walkers from the water, then leaps onto their backs and lets itself be carried as a crushing load. As soon as the victim reaches inhabited places, the spirit suddenly vanishes. Its name comes from the sound it makes when it rises out of the water.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "flodder-c01",
        "flodder-c02",
        "flodder-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Wujudnya di antara roh dan hewan: tidak bertubuh tetapi terlihat, sehingga tak dapat disentuh, namun sangat berat di punggung korban. Dalam beberapa legenda Brabant ia muncul sebagai anjing hitam besar yang disebut anjing neraka. Ia berkerabat dengan Lange Wapper dan Kludde dari Flandria, Blauwe Gerrit dari Veluwe, Ossaert dari Zeeland, dan Stoep dari Gelderland, serta mirip Bahkauv dari Aachen.",
      "en": "Its form lies between spirit and beast: bodiless but visible, so it cannot be touched, yet hugely heavy on the victim's back. In some Brabant legends it appears as a large black dog called a hellhound. It is related to Lange Wapper and the Kludde of Flanders, Blauwe Gerrit of the Veluwe, the Ossaert of Zeeland and the Stoep of Gelderland, and resembles the Bahkauv of Aachen.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "flodder-c04",
        "flodder-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "quote": "De Flodder (ook wel Flodderduivel) is een kwelgeest uit de West-Brabantse folklore.",
      "locator": null,

      "statement": {
        "id": "Flodder (juga Flodderduivel) adalah roh pengganggu dari cerita rakyat Brabant Barat (West-Brabantse), Belanda.",
        "en": "The Flodder (also Flodderduivel) is a tormenting spirit of West Brabant (West-Brabantse) folklore, in the Netherlands."
      }

    {
      "id": "flodder-c02",
      "source_id": "flodder-s1",
      "quote": "Het wezen houdt zich op in sloten, plassen en langs dijken. Het loert vanuit het water langs de kant op nachtelijke wandelaars, bespringt hen op de rug en laat zich vervolgens als een loodzware last meedragen. Zodra het slachtoffer de bewoonde wereld bereikt, verdwijnt de demon plots weer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini tinggal di parit, kolam, dan sepanjang tanggul; ia mengintai pejalan malam dari air, melompat ke punggung mereka dan minta digendong sebagai beban seberat timah; begitu korban mencapai permukiman, ia tiba-tiba lenyap.",
        "en": "The creature lives in ditches, pools and along dykes; it lurks in the water for night walkers, leaps on their backs and is carried as a lead-heavy load; once the victim reaches inhabited parts, it suddenly vanishes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flodder-c03",
      "source_id": "flodder-s1",
      "quote": "Zijn naam dankt de Flodder aan het geluid dat hij voortbrengt wanneer hij uit het water oprijst.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Flodder mendapat namanya dari bunyi yang ia timbulkan saat muncul dari air.",
        "en": "The Flodder owes its name to the sound it makes when rising from the water."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flodder-c04",
      "source_id": "flodder-s1",
      "quote": "Het houdt het midden tussen een geest en een beest. Niet stoffelijk, maar wel zichtbaar. In sommige Brabantse sagen verschijnt de plaaggeest in de gedaante van een grote, zwarte hond, die wordt aangeduid als helhond.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Flodder berada di antara roh dan hewan, tak berwujud jasmani tetapi terlihat, sehingga tak dapat disentuh namun sangat berat di punggung korban; dalam beberapa legenda Brabant (Brabantse) ia muncul sebagai anjing hitam besar yang disebut anjing neraka (helhond).",
        "en": "The Flodder is halfway between ghost and beast, immaterial yet visible, untouchable yet enormously heavy on the victim's back; in some Brabant (Brabantse) tales it appears as a large black dog called a hellhound (helhond)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flodder-c05",
      "source_id": "flodder-s2",
      "quote": "De Flodder of de Flodderduivel is in kwelgeast út 'e folkloare fan it westlike part fan Noard-Brabân.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {

        "en": "The Frisian article also calls the Flodder or Flodderduivel a tormenting spirit of the folklore of western North Brabant (Noard-Brabân)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flodder-c06",
      "source_id": "flodder-s1",
      "quote": "Het wezen is verwant aan Lange Wapper en de Kludde uit Vlaanderen, Blauwe Gerrit van de Veluwe, de Ossaert uit Zeeland en de Stoep uit Gelderland. Ook het Bahkauv uit Aken vertoont overeenkomsten.",
      "locator": null,

      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini berkerabat dengan Lange Wapper dan Kludde dari Flandria (Vlaanderen), Blauwe Gerrit dari Veluwe, Ossaert dari Zeeland, dan Stoep dari Gelderland; Bahkauv dari Aachen (Aken) juga mirip.",
        "en": "It is related to Lange Wapper and the Kludde of Flanders (Vlaanderen), Blauwe Gerrit of the Veluwe, the Ossaert of Zeeland and the Stoep of Gelderland; the Bahkauv of Aachen (Aken) is similar too."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Flogdrake (\"naga terbang\") adalah jenis naga dalam kepercayaan rakyat Swedia: naga cacing tanpa sayap yang melesat bercahaya di langit seperti bintang jatuh dan tinggal di terowongan gunung yang dibornya sendiri.",
    "en": "The flogdrake ('fly-dragon') is a type of dragon in Swedish folk belief: a wingless worm-dragon that streaks glowing across the sky like a shooting star and lives in mountain tunnels it bores itself.",
    "claim_ids": [

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Flogdrake (Swedia Kuno floghdraki), kadang disebut elddrake (\"naga api\") atau godsdrake, adalah jenis naga yang terutama berasal dari kepercayaan rakyat Swedia, dan dalam kadar lebih kecil dari cerita rakyat Finlandia dan Estonia. Ia naga cacing tanpa sayap yang terbang melintasi langit sambil menyala, tampak seperti garis api atau cahaya seperti bintang jatuh, dan tinggal di terowongan gunung yang dibornya sendiri.",
      "en": "The flogdrake (Old Swedish floghdraki), sometimes called elddrake ('fire dragon') or godsdrake, is a dragon type rooted mainly in Swedish folk belief and, to a lesser degree, in Finnish and Estonian lore. This limbless worm-dragon has no wings, yet it crosses the sky aglow like a fiery or luminous trail resembling a falling star, and it makes its home in tunnels it drills through mountains.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "flogdrake-c01",
        "flogdrake-c02"
      ]

    {
      "id": "Nama floghdraki berarti \"naga terbang\" dan merupakan pinjaman sastra dari kata Nordik Kuno flugdreki. Dari bahasa Swedia, kata ini dipinjam ke bahasa Finlandia sebagai louhikäärme, \"ular louhi\". Laurentius Petri mungkin sudah memakainya pada 1568 untuk menerjemahkan \"seraf\" dalam Yesaya 14:29 sebagai \"en brinnande floghdrake\".",
      "en": "The name floghdraki means 'fly-dragon' and is a literary loan from Old Norse flugdreki. From Swedish it was borrowed into Finnish as louhikäärme, 'louhi serpent'. Laurentius Petri may already have used it in 1568 to render 'seraph' in Isaiah 14:29 as 'en brinnande floghdrake'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "flogdrake-c03",
        "flogdrake-c04",
        "flogdrake-c09",
        "flogdrake-c12"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Berbeda dengan naga penjaga harta, flogdrake jarang terlihat dalam wujud aslinya; ia lebih sering tampak melesat di langit malam dikelilingi bunga api, seperti pita keemasan. Ia besar, tampak tak berkaki, dan bentuknya konon mirip timbangan dacin, seperti berudu panjang. Sebagian orang menafsirkan nyala itu sebagai naga yang menerangi hartanya dan menunjukkan letak harta atau sarangnya; flogdrake konon menyimpan diri dan hartanya di terowongan (drakarör) di dalam gunung.",
      "en": "Unlike the treasure-guarding dragon, the flogdrake is rarely seen in its true form; it is more often glimpsed streaking across the evening sky surrounded by sparks, like a golden ribbon. It is large, seemingly limbless, and said to resemble a steelyard balance, like a long tadpole.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c01",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s1",
      "quote": "is a type of dragon in Swedish folklore, and to a lesser extent Finnish and Estonian folklore (see kratt), by analogy a variation of the Pan-European firedrake myth; it is a wingless worm-dragon that flies across the sky while glowing, looking like a streak of fire or light akin to a shooting star, while living in mountain tunnels that it bores.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Flogdrake (Swedia Kuno floghdraki; Finlandia louhikäärme; juga elddrake atau godsdrake) adalah jenis naga dalam cerita rakyat Swedia (Swedish), dan sedikit dalam cerita rakyat Finlandia (Finnish) dan Estonia (Estonian): naga cacing tanpa sayap yang terbang bercahaya di langit seperti garis api atau bintang jatuh, dan tinggal di terowongan gunung yang dibornya.",
        "en": "The flogdrake (Old Swedish floghdraki; Finnish louhikäärme; also elddrake or godsdrake) is a dragon type of Swedish folklore, and less so Finnish and Estonian: a wingless worm-dragon flying glowing across the sky like a streak of fire or a shooting star, living in mountain tunnels it bores."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c02",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s2",
      "quote": "är en nordisk typ av drake som huvudsakligen härrör svensk folktro och folkminne, därtill även estsvensk folktro. Det är en vinglös ormdrake som flyger likt en eldstrimma eller ljusfenomen över himlen likt ett stjärnfall och bor i bergstunnlar de själva borrat.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Flogdrake adalah jenis naga Nordik (nordisk) yang terutama berasal dari kepercayaan rakyat Swedia (svensk), juga Swedia-Estonia; naga ular tanpa sayap yang terbang seperti garis api di langit seperti bintang jatuh dan tinggal di terowongan gunung yang dibornya sendiri.",
        "en": "The flogdrake is a Nordic (nordisk) dragon type chiefly from Swedish (svensk) folk belief, also Estonian-Swedish; a wingless serpent-dragon flying like a fire streak across the sky like a falling star, dwelling in mountain tunnels it bored itself."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c03",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s1",
      "quote": "Swedish: flogdrake derives from Old Swedish: floghdraki (including the form flughdraki), meaning ”fly-dragon”, and initially referred to just flying dragons, as opposed to non-flying ones. The term is a literary loan from Old Norse: flugdreki .",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata Swedia flogdrake berasal dari Swedia Kuno floghdraki (flughdraki), \"naga terbang\", semula untuk naga yang terbang saja, dan merupakan pinjaman sastra dari Nordik Kuno (Old Norse) flugdreki.",
        "en": "Swedish flogdrake derives from Old Swedish floghdraki (flughdraki), 'fly-dragon', at first only for flying dragons, a literary loan from Old Norse flugdreki."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c04",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s1",
      "quote": "From Old Swedish, the term was borrowed into Finnish as a partial calque, there becoming louhikäärme , \"louhi serpent\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dari Swedia Kuno, istilah ini dipinjam ke bahasa Finlandia (Finnish) sebagai kalke parsial louhikäärme, \"ular louhi\".",
        "en": "From Old Swedish the term entered Finnish as a partial calque, louhikäärme, 'louhi serpent'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c05",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s1",
      "quote": "Thus the flogdrake can be compared to the eastern dragon in that it can fly despite lacking any visible wings. Unlike eastern dragons, however, Germanic dragons are always portrayed as evil. It is said to come flying in the evening sky surrounded by sparks, looking like a golden stripe that sweeps across the sky.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Flogdrake mirip naga timur karena bisa terbang tanpa sayap yang terlihat, tetapi naga Jermanik selalu digambarkan jahat; ia datang terbang di langit senja dikelilingi bunga api, tampak seperti garis keemasan yang menyapu langit.",
        "en": "The flogdrake resembles the eastern dragon in flying without visible wings, but Germanic dragons are always evil; it comes flying in the evening sky amid sparks, like a golden stripe sweeping across it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c06",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s1",
      "quote": "It is large and seems to lack limbs, but is said to resemble a steelyard balance in shape, essentially like a long tadpole.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia besar dan tampak tak berkaki, dan bentuknya konon mirip timbangan dacin, pada dasarnya seperti berudu panjang.",
        "en": "It is large and seemingly limbless, said to be shaped like a steelyard balance, essentially a long tadpole."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c07",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s1",
      "quote": "The flogdrakes are said to have lived in mountains. They would drill into the rock mass, thus digging large tunnels, so-called drakarör",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Flogdrake konon tinggal di gunung, mengebor batuan dan menggali terowongan besar yang disebut drakarör, tempat mereka bersemayam dan menyembunyikan hartanya.",
        "en": "Flogdrakes are said to have lived in mountains, drilling into the rock and digging large tunnels called drakarör, where they dwelt and hid their treasure."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c08",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s1",
      "quote": "Some interpret the fire streak phenomenon as the dragon \"firing over his possessions\" or \"illuminating his goods\", and thus pointing out where the treasure or dragon's nest is to be found.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian orang menafsirkan garis api itu sebagai naga yang \"menerangi hartanya\", sehingga menunjukkan letak harta atau sarang naga.",
        "en": "Some read the fire streak as the dragon 'illuminating his goods', so revealing where the treasure or nest lies."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c09",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s2",
      "quote": "Laurentius Petri använde begreppet i sin senare betydelse kanske redan 1568 i sin översättning av Jesaja",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laurentius Petri mungkin sudah memakai istilah ini dalam arti kemudiannya pada 1568, dalam terjemahan Yesaya (Jesaja).",
        "en": "Laurentius Petri perhaps used the term in its later sense as early as 1568, in his translation of Isaiah (Jesaja)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c10",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s2",
      "quote": "Genom folketymologi har louhikäärme sedermera blivit lohikäärme (bokstavligen ”laxorm”) som på modern finska är ordet för drake i allmänhet.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Melalui etimologi rakyat, louhikäärme kemudian menjadi lohikäärme (harfiah \"ular salmon\"), kata Finlandia modern (finska) untuk naga pada umumnya.",
        "en": "Through folk etymology louhikäärme later became lohikäärme (literally 'salmon serpent'), the modern Finnish (finska) word for dragon in general."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c11",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s1",
      "quote": "This mythological pattern also occurs elsewhere, such as in the Baltic states and Northern Germany, where gnomes, such as the German-Slavic Schrat/Puk , and Estonian derivative kratt/puuk (Estonian Swedish: skrat), appear at night as a \"fiery dragon\" or thereof.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pola mitologis ini juga ada di negara Baltik (Baltic) dan Jerman Utara (Northern Germany), tempat roh seperti Schrat/Puk Jerman-Slavia dan kratt Estonia (Estonian) muncul pada malam hari sebagai \"naga api\".",
        "en": "This pattern also occurs in the Baltic states and Northern Germany, where gnomes like the German-Slavic Schrat/Puk and Estonian kratt appear at night as a 'fiery dragon'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c12",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s1",
      "quote": "in which Petri translated the last part as: \"en brinnande floghdrake\" (\"a burning fly-dragon\").",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Petri menerjemahkan bagian akhir Yesaya 14:29 (seraf) sebagai \"en brinnande floghdrake\" (\"naga terbang yang menyala\").",
        "en": "Petri rendered the end of Isaiah 14:29 (the seraph) as 'en brinnande floghdrake' ('a burning fly-dragon')."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flogdrake-c13",
      "source_id": "flogdrake-s1",
      "quote": "The flogdrake, as opposed to the treasure-guarding dragon, \"is rarely seen in its true form, but more often observed streaking through the air like a flame\", wrote Leonhard Fredrik Rääf (1856).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Berbeda dari naga penjaga harta, flogdrake \"jarang terlihat dalam wujud aslinya, lebih sering tampak melesat di udara seperti nyala api\", tulis Leonhard Fredrik Rääf (1856).",
        "en": "Unlike the treasure-guarding dragon, the flogdrake 'is rarely seen in its true form, but more often observed streaking through the air like a flame', wrote Leonhard Fredrik Rääf (1856)."
      }
    }
  ],

      "id": "Galar (Nordik Kuno Galarr) adalah kurcaci dalam mitologi Nordik.       "en": "Galar (Old Norse Galarr) is a dwarf of Norse mythology.     {
      "id": "Kemudian kedua kurcaci itu menyebabkan kematian raksasa Gillingr dan istrinya, sehingga putra mereka, Suttungr, menuntut mead tersebut sebagai tebusan. Nama Galar berarti \"yang berteriak\". Filolog Austria Rudolf Simek berpendapat nama Galar dan Fjalar adalah ciptaan Snorri sendiri.",
      "en": "Later the two dwarfs caused the death of the giant Gillingr and his wife, so their son Suttungr demanded the mead as compensation. The name Galar means 'the one who screams'. The Austrian philologist Rudolf Simek holds that the names Galar and Fjalar were invented by Snorri.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "galar-c01",
      "source_id": "galar-s1",
      "quote": "Fjalar og Galar eru dvergar í norrænni goðafræði. Þeir myrtu Kvasi og brugguðu skáldskaparmjöðinn úr blóði hans og hunangi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fjalar dan Galar adalah kurcaci dalam mitologi Nordik (norrænni); mereka membunuh Kvasir (Kvasi) dan membuat mead puisi dari darahnya dan madu.",
        "en": "Fjalar and Galar are dwarfs in Norse (norrænni) mythology; they murdered Kvasir (Kvasi) and brewed the mead of poetry from his blood and honey."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "galar-c02",
      "source_id": "galar-s2",
      "quote": "Galar (norrønt: Galarr) var i norrøn mytologi en dverg. Sammen med broren Fjalar drepte han den viseste av alle, skapt av fredspakten mellom Vaner og Æser, Kvase. Av hans blod blandet med honning laget de skaldskapsmjøden",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Galar (Nordik Kuno Galarr) adalah kurcaci dalam mitologi Nordik; bersama saudaranya Fjalar ia membunuh Kvasir (Kvase), makhluk paling bijak yang tercipta dari perjanjian damai Vanir (Vaner) dan Æsir (Æser), dan dari darahnya yang dicampur madu mereka membuat mead puisi.",
        "en": "Galar (Old Norse Galarr) was a dwarf in Norse mythology; with his brother Fjalar he killed Kvasir (Kvase), the wisest of all, born of the peace pact between the Vanir (Vaner) and Æsir (Æser), and from his blood mixed with honey they made the mead of poetry."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "galar-c03",
      "source_id": "galar-s1",
      "quote": "Síðar, er þeir höfðu valdið dauða jötunsins Gillings og konu hans, þá krafðist Suttungur sonur þeirra, mjaðarins í bætur.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kemudian, setelah mereka menyebabkan kematian raksasa Gillingr (Gillings) dan istrinya, putra mereka Suttungr (Suttungur) menuntut mead itu sebagai tebusan.",
        "en": "Later, after they caused the deaths of the giant Gillingr (Gillings) and his wife, their son Suttungr (Suttungur) demanded the mead as compensation."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "galar-c04",
      "source_id": "galar-s1",
      "quote": "Nafnið Fjalar er talið þýða „felur“ eða „svikari“ en Galar „sá sem öskrar“.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Fjalar dianggap berarti \"penyembunyi\" atau \"penipu\", dan Galar \"yang berteriak\".",
        "en": "The name Fjalar is taken to mean 'hider' or 'deceiver', and Galar 'the one who screams'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "galar-c05",
      "source_id": "galar-s2",
      "quote": "Den østerrikske filologen Rudolf Simek hevder at navnene Galar og Fjalar er konstruert av Snorre.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Filolog Austria Rudolf Simek berpendapat nama Galar dan Fjalar diciptakan oleh Snorri (Snorre).",
        "en": "Austrian philologist Rudolf Simek argues that the names Galar and Fjalar were constructed by Snorri (Snorre)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "galar-c06",
      "source_id": "galar-s2",
      "quote": "Historien om opphavet til skaldskapen og Suttungs mjød er fortalt i Snorre Sturlasons Gylvaginning.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah asal-usul puisi dan mead Suttungr diceritakan dalam Gylfaginning (Gylvaginning) karya Snorri Sturluson (Snorre Sturlasons).",
        "en": "The story of the origin of poetry and Suttungr's mead is told in Snorri Sturluson's (Snorre Sturlasons) Gylfaginning (Gylvaginning)."
      }
    }
  ],
